Implement the following plan:

# Fix `entire clean` logging and branch deletion bugs

## Context

Two bugs in `entire clean`:
1. Structured logs (`2026/02/10 12:43:25 INFO deleted orphaned shadow branch...`) leak to the terminal instead of going to `.entire/logs/entire.log`
2. `--force` reports branches as deleted but they remain — go-git v5's `RemoveReference()` doesn't work with packed refs/worktrees

## Bug 1: Logging to stdio

**Root cause**: `logging.Init()` is never called for the `clean` command. `getLogger()` falls back to `slog.Default()` which writes text-format logs to stderr.

**Fix**: Call `logging.Init("")` (empty session ID) at the start of `runClean()` and defer `logging.Close()`.

**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/clean.go:49` — `runClean()`

## Bug 2: Shadow branches not deleted

**Root cause**: `repo.Storer.RemoveReference()` doesn't properly persist deletions when refs are packed (`.git/packed-refs`) or in a worktree context. This is the same class of go-git v5 bug documented in CLAUDE.md for `reset --hard` and `checkout`.

Tests pass because they use `git.PlainInit` which creates loose refs — `RemoveReference` works for those.

**Fix**: Use `git branch -D <branch>` via CLI, consistent with how `HardResetWithProtection()` and `CheckoutBranch()` bypass go-git.

### Changes

1. **Add `DeleteBranchCLI()` helper** in `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go`
   - `func DeleteBranchCLI(branchName string) error` — runs `git branch -D <branch>`
   - Pattern matches `HardResetWithProtection()` at line 1112

2. **Update all non-test `RemoveReference` call sites** to use the new helper:

   | File | Line | Function | Notes |
   |------|------|----------|-------|
   | `strategy/cleanup.go` | 142 | `DeleteShadowBranches()` | Used by `entire clean` |
   | `strategy/manual_commit_git.go` | 362 | `deleteShadowBranch()` | Used by condensation |
   | `strategy/manual_commit_reset.go` | 81 | `Reset()` | Used by `entire reset` |
   | `strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` | 620 | `cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused()` | Post-commit cleanup |
   | `strategy/manual_commit_migration.go` | 67 | shadow branch rename | Delete old after creating new |
   | `cli/sessions_fix.go` | 291 | `fixOrphanedSession()` | Session fix command |
   | `checkpoint/temporary.go` | 618 | `GitStore.DeleteShadowBranch()` | Dead code (no callers), fix for correctness |

3. **Update test for `DeleteShadowBranches`** in `strategy/clean_test.go` — verify deletion via `git branch --list` CLI instead of go-git `repo.Reference()` to test the real code path.

### Scope note
`sessions_fix.go` is in the `cli` package, not `strategy`. It can call `strategy.DeleteBranchCLI()` since `cli` imports `strategy`. For `checkpoint/temporary.go`, it's in the `checkpoint` package — either add a similar helper there or make it call out to git CLI directly (it's dead code anyway).

## Verification

```bash
mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci
```

Then manual test: create a repo with packed refs + shadow branches, run `entire clean -f`, verify branches are actually gone with `git branch -l 'entire/*'`.

    20→
    21→const (
    22→	// sessionGracePeriod is the minimum age a session must have before it can be
    23→	// considered orphaned. This protects active sessions that haven't created
    24→	// their first checkpoint yet.
    25→	sessionGracePeriod = 10 * time.Minute
    26→)
    27→
    28→// CleanupType identifies the type of orphaned item.
    29→type CleanupType string
    30→
    31→const (
    32→	CleanupTypeShadowBranch CleanupType = "shadow-branch"
    33→	CleanupTypeSessionState CleanupType = "session-state"
    34→	CleanupTypeCheckpoint   CleanupType = "checkpoint"
    35→)
    36→
    37→// CleanupItem represents an orphaned item that can be cleaned up.
    38→type CleanupItem struct {
    39→	Type   CleanupType
    40→	ID     string // Branch name, session ID, or checkpoint ID
    41→	Reason string // Why this item is considered orphaned
    42→}
    43→
    44→// CleanupResult contains the results of a cleanup operation.
    45→type CleanupResult struct {
    46→	ShadowBranches    []string // Deleted shadow branches
    47→	SessionStates     []string // Deleted session state files
    48→	Checkpoints       []string // Deleted checkpoint metadata
    49→	FailedBranches    []string // Shadow branches that failed to delete
    50→	FailedStates      []string // Session states that failed to delete
    51→	FailedCheckpoints []string // Checkpoints that failed to delete
    52→}
    53→
    54→// shadowBranchPattern matches shadow branch names in both old and new formats:
    55→//   - Old format: entire/<commit[:7+]>
    56→//   - New format: entire/<commit[:7+]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>
    57→//
    58→// The pattern requires at least 7 hex characters for the commit, optionally followed
    59→// by a dash and exactly 6 hex characters for the worktree hash.
    60→var shadowBranchPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^entire/[0-9a-fA-F]{7,}(-[0-9a-fA-F]{6})?$`)
    61→
    62→// IsShadowBranch returns true if the branch name matches the shadow branch pattern.
    63→// Shadow branches have the format "entire/<commit-hash>-<worktree-hash>" where the
    64→// commit hash is at least 7 hex characters and worktree hash is 6 hex characters.
    65→// The "entire/checkpoints/v1" branch is NOT a shadow branch.
    66→func IsShadowBranch(branchName string) bool {
    67→	// Explicitly exclude entire/checkpoints/v1
    68→	if branchName == paths.MetadataBranchName {
    69→		return false
    70→	}
    71→	return shadowBranchPattern.MatchString(branchName)
    72→}
    73→
    74→// ListShadowBranches returns all shadow branches in the repository.
    75→// Shadow branches match the pattern "entire/<commit-hash>" (7+ hex chars).
    76→// The "entire/checkpoints/v1" branch is excluded as it stores permanent metadata.
    77→// Returns an empty slice (not nil) if no shadow branches exist.
    78→func ListShadowBranches() ([]string, error) {
    79→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	refs, err := repo.References()
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get references: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	var shadowBranches []string
    90→
    91→	err = refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
    92→		// Only look at branch references
    93→		if !ref.Name().IsBranch() {
    94→			return nil
    95→		}
    96→
    97→		// Extract branch name without refs/heads/ prefix
    98→		branchName := strings.TrimPrefix(ref.Name().String(), "refs/heads/")
    99→
   100→		if IsShadowBranch(branchName) {
   101→			shadowBranches = append(shadowBranches, branchName)
   102→		}
   103→		return nil
   104→	})
   105→	if err != nil {
   106→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate references: %w", err)
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	// Ensure we return empty slice, not nil
   110→	if shadowBranches == nil {
   111→		shadowBranches = []string{}
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	return shadowBranches, nil
   115→}
   116→
   117→// DeleteShadowBranches deletes the specified branches from the repository.
   118→// Returns two slices: successfully deleted branches and branches that failed to delete.
   119→// Individual branch deletion failures do not stop the operation - all branches are attempted.
   120→// Returns an error only if the repository cannot be opened.
   121→func DeleteShadowBranches(branches []string) (deleted []string, failed []string, err error) {
   122→	if len(branches) == 0 {
   123→		return []string{}, []string{}, nil
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   127→	if err != nil {
   128→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	for _, branch := range branches {
   132→		refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branch)
   133→
   134→		// Check if reference exists before trying to delete
   135→		ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   136→		if err != nil {
   137→			failed = append(failed, branch)
   138→			continue
   139→		}
   140→
   141→		// Delete the reference
   142→		if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(ref.Name()); err != nil {
   143→			failed = append(failed, branch)
   144→			continue
   145→		}
   146→
   147→		deleted = append(deleted, branch)
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	return deleted, failed, nil
   151→}
   152→
   153→// ListOrphanedSessionStates returns session state files that are orphaned.
   154→// A session state is orphaned if:
   155→//   - No checkpoints on entire/checkpoints/v1 reference this session ID
   156→//   - No shadow branch exists for the session's base commit
   157→//
   158→// This is strategy-agnostic as session states are shared by all strategies.
   159→func ListOrphanedSessionStates() ([]CleanupItem, error) {
   160→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Get all session states
   166→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
   167→	if err != nil {
   168→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
   172→	if err != nil {
   173→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	if len(states) == 0 {
   177→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	// Get all checkpoints to find which sessions have checkpoints
   181→	cpStore := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
   182→
   183→	sessionsWithCheckpoints := make(map[string]bool)
   184→	checkpoints, listErr := cpStore.ListCommitted(context.Background())
   185→	if listErr == nil {
   186→		for _, cp := range checkpoints {
   187→			sessionsWithCheckpoints[cp.SessionID] = true
   188→		}
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Get all shadow branches as a set for quick lookup
   192→	shadowBranches, _ := ListShadowBranches() //nolint:errcheck // Best effort
   193→	shadowBranchSet := make(map[string]bool)
   194→	for _, branch := range shadowBranches {
   195→		shadowBranchSet[branch] = true
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	var orphaned []CleanupItem
   199→	now := time.Now()
   200→
   201→	for _, state := range states {
   202→		// Skip sessions that started recently - they may be actively in use
   203→		// but haven't created their first checkpoint yet
   204→		if now.Sub(state.StartedAt) < sessionGracePeriod {
   205→			continue
   206→		}
   207→
   208→		// Check if session has checkpoints on entire/checkpoints/v1
   209→		hasCheckpoints := sessionsWithCheckpoints[state.SessionID]
   210→
   211→		// Check if shadow branch exists for this session's base commit and worktree
   212→		// Shadow branches are now worktree-specific: entire/<commit[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>
   213→		expectedBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   214→		hasShadowBranch := shadowBranchSet[expectedBranch]
   215→
   216→		// Session is orphaned if it has no checkpoints AND no shadow branch
   217→		if !hasCheckpoints && !hasShadowBranch {
   218→			reason := "no checkpoints or shadow branch found"
   219→			orphaned = append(orphaned, CleanupItem{
   220→				Type:   CleanupTypeSessionState,
   221→				ID:     state.SessionID,
   222→				Reason: reason,
   223→			})
   224→		}
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	return orphaned, nil
   228→}
   229→
   230→// DeleteOrphanedSessionStates deletes the specified session state files.
   231→func DeleteOrphanedSessionStates(sessionIDs []string) (deleted []string, failed []string, err error) {
   232→	if len(sessionIDs) == 0 {
   233→		return []string{}, []string{}, nil
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
   237→	if err != nil {
   238→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	for _, sessionID := range sessionIDs {
   242→		if err := store.Clear(context.Background(), sessionID); err != nil {
   243→			failed = append(failed, sessionID)
   244→		} else {
   245→			deleted = append(deleted, sessionID)
   246→		}
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	return deleted, failed, nil
   250→}
   251→
   252→// DeleteOrphanedCheckpoints removes checkpoint directories from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   253→func DeleteOrphanedCheckpoints(checkpointIDs []string) (deleted []string, failed []string, err error) {
   254→	if len(checkpointIDs) == 0 {
   255→		return []string{}, []string{}, nil
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   259→	if err != nil {
   260→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   261→	}
   262→
   263→	// Get sessions branch
   264→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   265→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   266→	if err != nil {
   267→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("sessions branch not found: %w", err)
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	parentCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   273→	}
   274→
   275→	baseTree, err := parentCommit.Tree()
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	// Flatten tree to entries
   281→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   282→	if err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(repo, baseTree, "", entries); err != nil {
   283→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to flatten tree: %w", err)
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	// Remove entries for each checkpoint
   287→	checkpointSet := make(map[string]bool)
   288→	for _, id := range checkpointIDs {
   289→		checkpointSet[id] = true
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	// Find and remove entries matching checkpoint paths
   293→	for path := range entries {
   294→		for checkpointIDStr := range checkpointSet {
   295→			cpID, err := id.NewCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   296→			if err != nil {
   297→				continue // Skip invalid checkpoint IDs
   298→			}
   299→			cpPath := cpID.Path()
   300→			if strings.HasPrefix(path, cpPath+"/") {
   301→				delete(entries, path)
   302→			}
   303→		}
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Build new tree
   307→	newTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(repo, entries)
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to build tree: %w", err)
   310→	}
   311→
   312→	// Create commit
   313→	commit := &object.Commit{
   314→		Author: object.Signature{
   315→			Name:  "Entire CLI",
   316→			Email: "cli@entire.io",
   317→			When:  parentCommit.Author.When,
   318→		},
   319→		Committer: object.Signature{
   320→			Name:  "Entire CLI",
   321→			Email: "cli@entire.io",
   322→			When:  parentCommit.Committer.When,
   323→		},
   324→		Message:      fmt.Sprintf("Cleanup: removed %d orphaned checkpoints", len(checkpointIDs)),
   325→		TreeHash:     newTreeHash,
   326→		ParentHashes: []plumbing.Hash{ref.Hash()},
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   330→	if err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {
   331→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to encode commit: %w", err)
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	commitHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
   335→	if err != nil {
   336→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to store commit: %w", err)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	// Update branch reference
   340→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
   341→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   342→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to update branch: %w", err)
   343→	}
   344→
   345→	// All checkpoints deleted successfully
   346→	return checkpointIDs, []string{}, nil
   347→}
   348→
   349→// ListAllCleanupItems returns all orphaned items across all categories.
   350→// It iterates over all registered strategies and calls ListOrphanedItems on those
   351→// that implement OrphanedItemsLister.
   352→// Returns an error if the repository cannot be opened.
   353→func ListAllCleanupItems() ([]CleanupItem, error) {
   354→	var items []CleanupItem
   355→	var firstErr error
   356→
   357→	// Iterate over all registered strategies
   358→	for _, name := range List() {
   359→		strat, err := Get(name)
   360→		if err != nil {
   361→			if firstErr == nil {
   362→				firstErr = err
   363→			}
   364→			continue
   365→		}
   366→
   367→		// Check if strategy implements OrphanedItemsLister
   368→		if lister, ok := strat.(OrphanedItemsLister); ok {
   369→			stratItems, err := lister.ListOrphanedItems()
   370→			if err != nil {
   371→				if firstErr == nil {
   372→					firstErr = err
   373→				}
   374→				continue
   375→			}
   376→			items = append(items, stratItems...)
   377→		}
   378→	}
   379→
   380→	// Orphaned session states (strategy-agnostic)
   381→	states, err := ListOrphanedSessionStates()
   382→	if err != nil {
   383→		if firstErr == nil {
   384→			firstErr = err
   385→		}
   386→	} else {
   387→		items = append(items, states...)
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	return items, firstErr
   391→}
   392→
   393→// DeleteAllCleanupItems deletes all specified cleanup items.
   394→// Logs each deletion for audit purposes.
   395→func DeleteAllCleanupItems(items []CleanupItem) (*CleanupResult, error) {
   396→	result := &CleanupResult{}
   397→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "cleanup")
   398→
   399→	// Build ID-to-Reason map for logging after deletion
   400→	reasonMap := make(map[string]string)
   401→	for _, item := range items {
   402→		reasonMap[item.ID] = item.Reason
   403→	}
   404→
   405→	// Group items by type
   406→	var branches, states, checkpoints []string
   407→	for _, item := range items {
   408→		switch item.Type {
   409→		case CleanupTypeShadowBranch:
   410→			branches = append(branches, item.ID)
   411→		case CleanupTypeSessionState:
   412→			states = append(states, item.ID)
   413→		case CleanupTypeCheckpoint:
   414→			checkpoints = append(checkpoints, item.ID)
   415→		}
   416→	}
   417→
   418→	// Delete shadow branches
   419→	if len(branches) > 0 {
   420→		deleted, failed, err := DeleteShadowBranches(branches)
   421→		if err != nil {
   422→			return result, err
   423→		}
   424→		result.ShadowBranches = deleted
   425→		result.FailedBranches = failed
   426→
   427→		// Log deleted branches
   428→		for _, id := range deleted {
   429→			logging.Info(logCtx, "deleted orphaned shadow branch",
   430→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeShadowBranch)),
   431→				slog.String("id", id),
   432→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   433→			)
   434→		}
   435→		// Log failed branches
   436→		for _, id := range failed {
   437→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "failed to delete orphaned shadow branch",
   438→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeShadowBranch)),
   439→				slog.String("id", id),
   440→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   441→			)
   442→		}
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	// Delete session states
   446→	if len(states) > 0 {
   447→		deleted, failed, err := DeleteOrphanedSessionStates(states)
   448→		if err != nil {
   449→			return result, err
   450→		}
   451→		result.SessionStates = deleted
   452→		result.FailedStates = failed
   453→
   454→		// Log deleted session states
   455→		for _, id := range deleted {
   456→			logging.Info(logCtx, "deleted orphaned session state",
   457→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeSessionState)),
   458→				slog.String("id", id),
   459→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   460→			)
   461→		}
   462→		// Log failed session states
   463→		for _, id := range failed {
   464→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "failed to delete orphaned session state",
   465→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeSessionState)),
   466→				slog.String("id", id),
   467→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   468→			)
   469→		}
   470→	}
   471→
   472→	// Delete checkpoints
   473→	if len(checkpoints) > 0 {
   474→		deleted, failed, err := DeleteOrphanedCheckpoints(checkpoints)
   475→		if err != nil {
   476→			return result, err
   477→		}
   478→		result.Checkpoints = deleted
   479→		result.FailedCheckpoints = failed
   480→
   481→		// Log deleted checkpoints
   482→		for _, id := range deleted {
   483→			logging.Info(logCtx, "deleted orphaned checkpoint",
   484→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeCheckpoint)),
   485→				slog.String("id", id),
   486→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   487→			)
   488→		}
   489→		// Log failed checkpoints
   490→		for _, id := range failed {
   491→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "failed to delete orphaned checkpoint",
   492→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeCheckpoint)),
   493→				slog.String("id", id),
   494→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   495→			)
   496→		}
   497→	}
   498→
   499→	// Log summary
   500→	totalDeleted := len(result.ShadowBranches) + len(result.SessionStates) + len(result.Checkpoints)
   501→	totalFailed := len(result.FailedBranches) + len(result.FailedStates) + len(result.FailedCheckpoints)
   502→	if totalDeleted > 0 || totalFailed > 0 {
   503→		logging.Info(logCtx, "cleanup completed",
   504→			slog.Int("deleted_branches", len(result.ShadowBranches)),
   505→			slog.Int("deleted_session_states", len(result.SessionStates)),
   506→			slog.Int("deleted_checkpoints", len(result.Checkpoints)),
   507→			slog.Int("failed_branches", len(result.FailedBranches)),
   508→			slog.Int("failed_session_states", len(result.FailedStates)),
   509→			slog.Int("failed_checkpoints", len(result.FailedCheckpoints)),
   510→		)
   511→	}
   512→
   513→	return result, nil
   514→}
   515→

    21→// SaveChanges saves a checkpoint to the shadow branch.
    22→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteTemporary for git operations.
    23→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) SaveChanges(ctx SaveContext) error {
    24→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    25→	if err != nil {
    26→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    27→	}
    28→
    29→	// Extract session ID from metadata dir
    30→	sessionID := filepath.Base(ctx.MetadataDir)
    31→
    32→	// Load or initialize session state
    33→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→	// Initialize if state is nil OR BaseCommit is empty (can happen with partial state from warnings)
    38→	if state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
    39→		agentType := resolveAgentType(ctx.AgentType, state)
    40→		state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, "", "") // No transcript/prompt in fallback
    41→		if err != nil {
    42→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
    43→		}
    44→	}
    45→
    46→	// Check if HEAD has changed (e.g., Claude did a rebase via tool call) and migrate if needed
    47→	if err := s.migrateAndPersistIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
    48→		return err
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	// Get checkpoint store
    52→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Check if shadow branch exists to report whether we created it
    58→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    59→	branchExisted := store.ShadowBranchExists(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    60→
    61→	// Use the pending attribution calculated at prompt start (in InitializeSession)
    62→	// This was calculated BEFORE the agent made changes, so it accurately captures user edits
    63→	var promptAttr PromptAttribution
    64→	if state.PendingPromptAttribution != nil {
    65→		promptAttr = *state.PendingPromptAttribution
    66→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil // Clear after use
    67→	} else {
    68→		// No pending attribution (e.g., first checkpoint or session initialized without it)
    69→		promptAttr = PromptAttribution{CheckpointNumber: state.StepCount + 1}
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Log the prompt attribution for debugging
    73→	attrLogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
    74→	logging.Debug(attrLogCtx, "prompt attribution at checkpoint save",
    75→		slog.Int("checkpoint_number", promptAttr.CheckpointNumber),
    76→		slog.Int("user_added", promptAttr.UserLinesAdded),
    77→		slog.Int("user_removed", promptAttr.UserLinesRemoved),
    78→		slog.Int("agent_added", promptAttr.AgentLinesAdded),
    79→		slog.Int("agent_removed", promptAttr.AgentLinesRemoved),
    80→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID))
    81→
    82→	// Use WriteTemporary to create the checkpoint
    83→	isFirstCheckpointOfSession := state.StepCount == 0
    84→	result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
    85→		SessionID:         sessionID,
    86→		BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,
    87→		WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,
    88→		ModifiedFiles:     ctx.ModifiedFiles,
    89→		NewFiles:          ctx.NewFiles,
    90→		DeletedFiles:      ctx.DeletedFiles,
    91→		MetadataDir:       ctx.MetadataDir,
    92→		MetadataDirAbs:    ctx.MetadataDirAbs,
    93→		CommitMessage:     ctx.CommitMessage,
    94→		AuthorName:        ctx.AuthorName,
    95→		AuthorEmail:       ctx.AuthorEmail,
    96→		IsFirstCheckpoint: isFirstCheckpointOfSession,
    97→	})
    98→	if err != nil {
    99→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write temporary checkpoint: %w", err)
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	// If checkpoint was skipped due to deduplication (no changes), return early
   103→	if result.Skipped {
   104→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   105→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
   106→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   107→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   108→			slog.Int("checkpoint_count", state.StepCount),
   109→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   110→		)
   111→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last checkpoint)\n")
   112→		return nil
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	// Update session state
   116→	state.StepCount++
   117→
   118→	// Note: PendingCheckpointID is intentionally NOT cleared here.
   119→	// It is set by PostCommit (ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED) and consumed by
   120→	// handleTurnEndCondense. Clearing it here would cause a mismatch between
   121→	// the checkpoint ID in the commit trailer and the condensed metadata.
   122→
   123→	// Store the prompt attribution we calculated before saving
   124→	state.PromptAttributions = append(state.PromptAttributions, promptAttr)
   125→
   126→	// Track touched files (modified, new, and deleted)
   127→	state.FilesTouched = mergeFilesTouched(state.FilesTouched, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
   128→
   129→	// On first checkpoint, record the transcript identifier for this session
   130→	if state.StepCount == 1 {
   131→		state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart = ctx.StepTranscriptIdentifier
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	// Accumulate token usage
   135→	if ctx.TokenUsage != nil {
   136→		state.TokenUsage = accumulateTokenUsage(state.TokenUsage, ctx.TokenUsage)
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Save updated state
   140→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   141→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	if !branchExisted {
   145→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created shadow branch '%s' and committed changes\n", shadowBranchName)
   146→	} else {
   147→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed changes to shadow branch '%s'\n", shadowBranchName)
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	// Log checkpoint creation
   151→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   152→	logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint saved",
   153→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   154→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   155→		slog.Int("checkpoint_count", state.StepCount),
   156→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   157→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   158→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   159→		slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   160→		slog.Bool("branch_created", !branchExisted),
   161→	)
   162→
   163→	return nil
   164→}

   166→// SaveTaskCheckpoint saves a task checkpoint to the shadow branch.
   167→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteTemporaryTask for git operations.
   168→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx TaskCheckpointContext) error {
   169→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   170→	if err != nil {
   171→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	// Load session state
   175→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(ctx.SessionID)
   176→	if err != nil || state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
   177→		agentType := resolveAgentType(ctx.AgentType, state)
   178→		state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, ctx.SessionID, agentType, "", "") // No transcript/prompt in fallback
   179→		if err != nil {
   180→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session for task checkpoint: %w", err)
   181→		}
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	// Check if HEAD has changed (e.g., Claude did a rebase via tool call) and migrate if needed
   185→	if err := s.migrateAndPersistIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
   186→		return err
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	// Get checkpoint store
   190→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Check if shadow branch exists to report whether we created it
   196→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   197→	branchExisted := store.ShadowBranchExists(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   198→
   199→	// Compute metadata paths for commit message
   200→	sessionMetadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(ctx.SessionID)
   201→	taskMetadataDir := TaskMetadataDir(sessionMetadataDir, ctx.ToolUseID)
   202→
   203→	// Generate commit message
   204→	shortToolUseID := ctx.ToolUseID
   205→	if len(shortToolUseID) > 12 {
   206→		shortToolUseID = shortToolUseID[:12]
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	var messageSubject string
   210→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   211→		messageSubject = FormatIncrementalSubject(
   212→			ctx.IncrementalType,
   213→			ctx.SubagentType,
   214→			ctx.TaskDescription,
   215→			ctx.TodoContent,
   216→			ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   217→			shortToolUseID,
   218→		)
   219→	} else {
   220→		messageSubject = FormatSubagentEndMessage(ctx.SubagentType, ctx.TaskDescription, shortToolUseID)
   221→	}
   222→	commitMsg := trailers.FormatShadowTaskCommit(
   223→		messageSubject,
   224→		taskMetadataDir,
   225→		ctx.SessionID,
   226→	)
   227→
   228→	// Use WriteTemporaryTask to create the checkpoint
   229→	_, err = store.WriteTemporaryTask(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryTaskOptions{
   230→		SessionID:              ctx.SessionID,
   231→		BaseCommit:             state.BaseCommit,
   232→		WorktreeID:             state.WorktreeID,
   233→		ToolUseID:              ctx.ToolUseID,
   234→		AgentID:                ctx.AgentID,
   235→		ModifiedFiles:          ctx.ModifiedFiles,
   236→		NewFiles:               ctx.NewFiles,
   237→		DeletedFiles:           ctx.DeletedFiles,
   238→		TranscriptPath:         ctx.TranscriptPath,
   239→		SubagentTranscriptPath: ctx.SubagentTranscriptPath,
   240→		CheckpointUUID:         ctx.CheckpointUUID,
   241→		CommitMessage:          commitMsg,
   242→		AuthorName:             ctx.AuthorName,
   243→		AuthorEmail:            ctx.AuthorEmail,
   244→		IsIncremental:          ctx.IsIncremental,
   245→		IncrementalSequence:    ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   246→		IncrementalType:        ctx.IncrementalType,
   247→		IncrementalData:        ctx.IncrementalData,
   248→	})
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write task checkpoint: %w", err)
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Track touched files (modified, new, and deleted)
   254→	state.FilesTouched = mergeFilesTouched(state.FilesTouched, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
   255→
   256→	// Save updated state
   257→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   258→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   259→	}
   260→
   261→	if !branchExisted {
   262→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created shadow branch '%s' and committed task checkpoint\n", shadowBranchName)
   263→	} else {
   264→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed task checkpoint to shadow branch '%s'\n", shadowBranchName)
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	// Log task checkpoint creation
   268→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   269→	attrs := []any{
   270→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   271→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "task"),
   272→		slog.String("checkpoint_uuid", ctx.CheckpointUUID),
   273→		slog.String("tool_use_id", ctx.ToolUseID),
   274→		slog.String("subagent_type", ctx.SubagentType),
   275→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   276→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   277→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   278→		slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   279→		slog.Bool("branch_created", !branchExisted),
   280→	}
   281→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   282→		attrs = append(attrs,
   283→			slog.Bool("is_incremental", true),
   284→			slog.String("incremental_type", ctx.IncrementalType),
   285→			slog.Int("incremental_sequence", ctx.IncrementalSequence),
   286→		)
   287→	}
   288→	logging.Info(logCtx, "task checkpoint saved", attrs...)
   289→
   290→	return nil
   291→}
   292→
   293→// mergeFilesTouched merges multiple file lists into existing touched files, deduplicating.
   294→func mergeFilesTouched(existing []string, fileLists ...[]string) []string {
   295→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   296→	for _, f := range existing {
   297→		seen[f] = true
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	for _, list := range fileLists {
   301→		for _, f := range list {
   302→			seen[f] = true
   303→		}
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	result := make([]string, 0, len(seen))
   307→	for f := range seen {
   308→		result = append(result, f)
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Sort for deterministic output
   312→	sort.Strings(result)
   313→	return result
   314→}
   315→
   316→// accumulateTokenUsage adds new token usage to existing accumulated usage.
   317→// If existing is nil, returns a copy of incoming. If incoming is nil, returns existing unchanged.
   318→func accumulateTokenUsage(existing, incoming *agent.TokenUsage) *agent.TokenUsage {
   319→	if incoming == nil {
   320→		return existing
   321→	}
   322→	if existing == nil {
   323→		// Return a copy to avoid sharing the pointer
   324→		return &agent.TokenUsage{
   325→			InputTokens:         incoming.InputTokens,
   326→			CacheCreationTokens: incoming.CacheCreationTokens,
   327→			CacheReadTokens:     incoming.CacheReadTokens,
   328→			OutputTokens:        incoming.OutputTokens,
   329→			APICallCount:        incoming.APICallCount,
   330→			SubagentTokens:      incoming.SubagentTokens,
   331→		}
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Accumulate values
   335→	existing.InputTokens += incoming.InputTokens
   336→	existing.CacheCreationTokens += incoming.CacheCreationTokens
   337→	existing.CacheReadTokens += incoming.CacheReadTokens
   338→	existing.OutputTokens += incoming.OutputTokens
   339→	existing.APICallCount += incoming.APICallCount
   340→
   341→	// Accumulate subagent tokens if present
   342→	if incoming.SubagentTokens != nil {
   343→		existing.SubagentTokens = accumulateTokenUsage(existing.SubagentTokens, incoming.SubagentTokens)
   344→	}
   345→
   346→	return existing
   347→}
   348→
   349→// deleteShadowBranch deletes a shadow branch by name using an existing repo handle.
   350→// Returns nil if the branch doesn't exist (idempotent).
   351→func deleteShadowBranch(repo *git.Repository, branchName string) error {
   352→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName)
   353→
   354→	// Check if reference exists
   355→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   356→	if err != nil {
   357→		// Branch doesn't exist - nothing to delete (idempotent)
   358→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Not an error condition - branch already gone
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	// Delete the reference
   362→	if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(ref.Name()); err != nil {
   363→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete branch %s: %w", branchName, err)
   364→	}
   365→
   366→	return nil
   367→}

    29→const (

    31→	branchMaster = "master"

    34→// errStop is a sentinel error used to break out of git log iteration.
    35→// Shared across strategies that iterate through git commits.
    36→// NOTE: A similar sentinel exists in checkpoint/temporary.go - this is intentional.
    37→// Each package needs its own package-scoped sentinel for git log iteration patterns.
    38→var errStop = errors.New("stop iteration")
    39→
    40→// IsAncestorOf checks if commit is an ancestor of (or equal to) target.
    41→// Returns true if target can reach commit by following parent links.
    42→// Limits search to 1000 commits to avoid excessive traversal.
    43→func IsAncestorOf(repo *git.Repository, commit, target plumbing.Hash) bool {
    44→	if commit == target {
    45→		return true
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: target})
    49→	if err != nil {
    50→		return false
    51→	}
    52→	defer iter.Close()
    53→
    54→	found := false
    55→	count := 0
    56→	_ = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort search, errors are non-fatal
    57→		count++
    58→		if count > 1000 {
    59→			return errStop
    60→		}
    61→		if c.Hash == commit {
    62→			found = true
    63→			return errStop
    64→		}
    65→		return nil
    66→	})
    67→
    68→	return found
    69→}
    70→
    71→// ListCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    72→// Scans sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/ directories containing metadata.json.
    73→// Used by both manual-commit and auto-commit strategies.
    74→func ListCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    75→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    76→	if err != nil {
    77→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
    81→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
    82→	if err != nil {
    83→		//nolint:nilerr // No sessions branch yet is expected, return empty list
    84→		return []CheckpointInfo{}, nil
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	var checkpoints []CheckpointInfo
    98→
    99→	// Scan sharded structure: <2-char-prefix>/<remaining-id>/metadata.json
   100→	// The tree has 2-character directories (hex buckets)
   101→	for _, bucketEntry := range tree.Entries {
   102→		if bucketEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   103→			continue
   104→		}
   105→		// Bucket should be 2 hex chars
   106→		if len(bucketEntry.Name) != 2 {
   107→			continue
   108→		}
   109→
   110→		bucketTree, treeErr := repo.TreeObject(bucketEntry.Hash)
   111→		if treeErr != nil {
   112→			continue
   113→		}
   114→
   115→		// Each entry in the bucket is the remaining part of the checkpoint ID
   116→		for _, checkpointEntry := range bucketTree.Entries {
   117→			if checkpointEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   118→				continue
   119→			}
   120→
   121→			checkpointTree, cpTreeErr := repo.TreeObject(checkpointEntry.Hash)
   122→			if cpTreeErr != nil {
   123→				continue
   124→			}
   125→
   126→			// Reconstruct checkpoint ID: <bucket><remaining>
   127→			checkpointIDStr := bucketEntry.Name + checkpointEntry.Name
   128→			checkpointID, cpErr := id.NewCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   129→			if cpErr != nil {
   130→				// Skip invalid checkpoint IDs
   131→				continue
   132→			}
   133→
   134→			info := CheckpointInfo{
   135→				CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   136→			}
   137→
   138→			// Get details from metadata file (CheckpointSummary format)
   139→			if metadataFile, fileErr := checkpointTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   140→				if content, contentErr := metadataFile.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   141→					var summary checkpoint.CheckpointSummary
   142→					if json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary) == nil && len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   143→						info.CheckpointsCount = summary.CheckpointsCount
   144→						info.FilesTouched = summary.FilesTouched
   145→						info.SessionCount = len(summary.Sessions)
   146→
   147→						// Read session-level metadata for Agent, SessionID, CreatedAt, SessionIDs
   148→						for i, sessionPaths := range summary.Sessions {
   149→							if sessionPaths.Metadata != "" {
   150→								// SessionFilePaths now contains absolute paths with leading "/"
   151→								// Strip the leading "/" for tree.File() which expects paths without leading slash
   152→								sessionMetadataPath := strings.TrimPrefix(sessionPaths.Metadata, "/")
   153→								if sessionFile, sErr := tree.File(sessionMetadataPath); sErr == nil {
   154→									if sessionContent, scErr := sessionFile.Contents(); scErr == nil {
   155→										var sessionMetadata checkpoint.CommittedMetadata
   156→										if json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &sessionMetadata) == nil {
   157→											info.SessionIDs = append(info.SessionIDs, sessionMetadata.SessionID)
   158→											// Use first session's metadata for Agent, SessionID, CreatedAt
   159→											if i == 0 {
   160→												info.Agent = sessionMetadata.Agent
   161→												info.SessionID = sessionMetadata.SessionID
   162→												info.CreatedAt = sessionMetadata.CreatedAt
   163→												info.IsTask = sessionMetadata.IsTask
   164→												info.ToolUseID = sessionMetadata.ToolUseID
   165→											}
   166→										}
   167→									}
   168→								}
   169→							}
   170→						}
   171→					}
   172→				}
   173→			}
   174→
   175→			checkpoints = append(checkpoints, info)
   176→		}
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	// Sort by time (most recent first)
   180→	sort.Slice(checkpoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   181→		return checkpoints[i].CreatedAt.After(checkpoints[j].CreatedAt)
   182→	})
   183→
   184→	return checkpoints, nil
   185→}

   190→	gitDir             = ".git"

   192→	shadowBranchPrefix = "entire/"

   194→	// DefaultAgentType is the generic fallback agent type name
   195→	DefaultAgentType = agent.AgentTypeUnknown

   197→
   198→// isSpecificAgentType returns true if the agent type is a known, specific value
   199→// (not empty and not the generic "Agent" fallback).
   200→func isSpecificAgentType(t agent.AgentType) bool {
   201→	return t != "" && t != DefaultAgentType
   202→}
   203→
   204→// resolveAgentType picks the best agent type from the context and existing state.
   205→// Priority: existing state (if specific) > context value > default fallback.
   206→func resolveAgentType(ctxAgentType agent.AgentType, state *SessionState) agent.AgentType {
   207→	if state != nil && isSpecificAgentType(state.AgentType) {
   208→		return state.AgentType
   209→	}
   210→	if ctxAgentType != "" {
   211→		return ctxAgentType
   212→	}
   213→	return DefaultAgentType
   214→}

   216→// ensureMetadataBranch creates the orphan entire/checkpoints/v1 branch if it doesn't exist.
   217→// This branch has no parent and starts with an empty tree.
   218→func EnsureMetadataBranch(repo *git.Repository) error {
   219→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   220→
   221→	// Check if branch already exists
   222→	_, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   223→	if err == nil {
   224→		// Branch already exists
   225→		return nil
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Create empty tree (no files)
   229→	emptyTree := &object.Tree{Entries: []object.TreeEntry{}}
   230→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   231→	if err := emptyTree.Encode(obj); err != nil {
   232→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode empty tree: %w", err)
   233→	}
   234→	emptyTreeHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
   235→	if err != nil {
   236→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to store empty tree: %w", err)
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	// Create orphan commit (no parent)
   240→	now := time.Now()
   241→	sig := object.Signature{
   242→		Name:  "Entire",
   243→		Email: "entire@local",
   244→		When:  now,
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	commit := &object.Commit{
   248→		TreeHash:  emptyTreeHash,
   249→		Author:    sig,
   250→		Committer: sig,
   251→		Message:   "Initialize metadata branch\n\nThis branch stores session metadata for the auto-commit strategy.\n",
   252→	}
   253→	// Note: No ParentHashes - this is an orphan commit
   254→
   255→	commitObj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   256→	if err := commit.Encode(commitObj); err != nil {
   257→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode orphan commit: %w", err)
   258→	}
   259→	commitHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(commitObj)
   260→	if err != nil {
   261→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to store orphan commit: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	// Create branch reference
   265→	ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
   266→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {
   267→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create metadata branch: %w", err)
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "✓ Created orphan branch '%s' for session metadata\n", paths.MetadataBranchName)
   271→	return nil
   272→}
   273→
   274→// readCheckpointMetadata reads metadata.json from a checkpoint path on entire/checkpoints/v1.
   275→// With the new format, root metadata.json is a CheckpointSummary with Agents array.
   276→// This function reads the summary and extracts relevant fields into CheckpointInfo,
   277→// also reading session-level metadata for IsTask/ToolUseID fields.
   278→func ReadCheckpointMetadata(tree *object.Tree, checkpointPath string) (*CheckpointInfo, error) {
   279→	metadataPath := checkpointPath + "/metadata.json"
   280→	file, err := tree.File(metadataPath)
   281→	if err != nil {
   282→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find metadata at %s: %w", metadataPath, err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	content, err := file.Contents()
   286→	if err != nil {
   287→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read metadata: %w", err)
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Try to parse as CheckpointSummary first (new format)
   291→	var summary checkpoint.CheckpointSummary
   292→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err == nil {
   293→		// If we have sessions array, this is the new format
   294→		if len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   295→			info := &CheckpointInfo{
   296→				CheckpointID:     summary.CheckpointID,
   297→				CheckpointsCount: summary.CheckpointsCount,
   298→				FilesTouched:     summary.FilesTouched,
   299→				SessionCount:     len(summary.Sessions),
   300→			}
   301→
   302→			// Read all sessions' metadata to populate SessionIDs and get other fields from first session
   303→			var sessionIDs []string
   304→			for i, sessionPaths := range summary.Sessions {
   305→				if sessionPaths.Metadata != "" {
   306→					// SessionFilePaths now contains absolute paths with leading "/"
   307→					// Strip the leading "/" for tree.File() which expects paths without leading slash
   308→					sessionMetadataPath := strings.TrimPrefix(sessionPaths.Metadata, "/")
   309→					if sessionFile, err := tree.File(sessionMetadataPath); err == nil {
   310→						if sessionContent, err := sessionFile.Contents(); err == nil {
   311→							var sessionMetadata checkpoint.CommittedMetadata
   312→							if json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &sessionMetadata) == nil {
   313→								sessionIDs = append(sessionIDs, sessionMetadata.SessionID)
   314→								// Use first session for Agent, SessionID, CreatedAt, IsTask, ToolUseID
   315→								if i == 0 {
   316→									info.Agent = sessionMetadata.Agent
   317→									info.SessionID = sessionMetadata.SessionID
   318→									info.CreatedAt = sessionMetadata.CreatedAt
   319→									info.IsTask = sessionMetadata.IsTask
   320→									info.ToolUseID = sessionMetadata.ToolUseID
   321→								}
   322→							}
   323→						}
   324→					}
   325→				}
   326→			}
   327→			info.SessionIDs = sessionIDs
   328→
   329→			return info, nil
   330→		}
   331→	}
   332→
   333→	// Fall back to parsing as CheckpointInfo (old format or direct info)
   334→	var metadata CheckpointInfo
   335→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &metadata); err != nil {
   336→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse metadata: %w", err)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	return &metadata, nil
   340→}

   342→// GetMetadataBranchTree returns the tree object for the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.

   344→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   345→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)

   348→	}
   349→
   350→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())

   355→	tree, err := commit.Tree()

   361→
   362→// ExtractFirstPrompt extracts and truncates the first meaningful prompt from prompt content.
   363→// Prompts are separated by "\n\n---\n\n". Skips empty prompts and separator-only content.
   364→// Returns empty string if no valid prompt is found.
   365→func ExtractFirstPrompt(content string) string {
   366→	if content == "" {
   367→		return ""
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	// Prompts are separated by "\n\n---\n\n"
   371→	// Find the first non-empty prompt
   372→	prompts := strings.Split(content, "\n\n---\n\n")
   373→	var firstPrompt string
   374→	for _, p := range prompts {
   375→		cleaned := strings.TrimSpace(p)
   376→		// Skip empty prompts or prompts that are just dashes/separators
   377→		if cleaned == "" || isOnlySeparators(cleaned) {
   378→			continue
   379→		}
   380→		firstPrompt = cleaned
   381→		break
   382→	}
   383→
   384→	if firstPrompt == "" {
   385→		return ""
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	return TruncateDescription(firstPrompt, MaxDescriptionLength)
   389→}
   390→
   391→// ReadSessionPromptFromTree reads the first meaningful prompt from a checkpoint's prompt.txt file in a git tree.
   392→// Returns an empty string if the prompt cannot be read.
   393→func ReadSessionPromptFromTree(tree *object.Tree, checkpointPath string) string {
   394→	promptPath := checkpointPath + "/" + paths.PromptFileName
   395→	file, err := tree.File(promptPath)
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		return ""
   398→	}
   399→
   400→	content, err := file.Contents()
   401→	if err != nil {
   402→		return ""
   403→	}
   404→
   405→	return ExtractFirstPrompt(content)
   406→}
   407→
   408→// isOnlySeparators checks if a string contains only dashes, spaces, and newlines.
   409→func isOnlySeparators(s string) bool {
   410→	for _, r := range s {
   411→		if r != '-' && r != ' ' && r != '\n' && r != '\r' && r != '\t' {
   412→			return false
   413→		}
   414→	}
   415→	return true
   416→}
   417→
   418→// ReadAllSessionPromptsFromTree reads the first prompt for all sessions in a multi-session checkpoint.
   419→// Returns a slice of prompts parallel to sessionIDs (oldest to newest).
   420→// For single-session checkpoints, returns a slice with just the root prompt.
   421→func ReadAllSessionPromptsFromTree(tree *object.Tree, checkpointPath string, sessionCount int, sessionIDs []string) []string {
   422→	if sessionCount <= 1 || len(sessionIDs) <= 1 {
   423→		// Single session - just return the root prompt
   424→		prompt := ReadSessionPromptFromTree(tree, checkpointPath)
   425→		if prompt != "" {
   426→			return []string{prompt}
   427→		}
   428→		return nil
   429→	}
   430→
   431→	// Multi-session: read prompts from archived folders (0/, 1/, etc.) and root
   432→	prompts := make([]string, len(sessionIDs))
   433→
   434→	// Read archived session prompts (folders 0, 1, ... N-2)
   435→	for i := range sessionCount - 1 {
   436→		archivedPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d", checkpointPath, i)
   437→		prompts[i] = ReadSessionPromptFromTree(tree, archivedPath)
   438→	}
   439→
   440→	// Read the most recent session prompt (at root level)
   441→	prompts[len(prompts)-1] = ReadSessionPromptFromTree(tree, checkpointPath)
   442→
   443→	return prompts
   444→}
   445→
   446→// ReadSessionPromptFromShadow reads the first prompt for a session from the shadow branch.
   447→// Returns an empty string if the prompt cannot be read.
   448→func ReadSessionPromptFromShadow(repo *git.Repository, baseCommit, worktreeID, sessionID string) string {
   449→	// Get shadow branch for this base commit using worktree-specific naming
   450→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   451→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName), true)
   452→	if err != nil {
   453→		return ""
   454→	}
   455→
   456→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   457→	if err != nil {
   458→		return ""
   459→	}
   460→
   461→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   462→	if err != nil {
   463→		return ""
   464→	}
   465→
   466→	// Build the path to prompt.txt: .entire/metadata/<session-id>/prompt.txt
   467→	checkpointPath := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + sessionID
   468→	return ReadSessionPromptFromTree(tree, checkpointPath)
   469→}

   472→func GetRemoteMetadataBranchTree(repo *git.Repository) (*object.Tree, error) {
   473→	refName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", paths.MetadataBranchName)
   474→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
