```
   490→
   491→// OpenRepository opens the git repository with linked worktree support enabled.
   492→// It uses git.PlainOpenWithOptions with EnableDotGitCommonDir set to true,
   493→// which is required for proper operation in git worktrees created via 'git worktree add'.
   494→//
   495→// Without EnableDotGitCommonDir, go-git operations in worktrees can silently fail:
   496→// - Commits appear to succeed but are not persisted
   497→// - Refs are written to incorrect locations
   498→// - The worktree's HEAD/index don't get updated properly
   499→//
   500→// This happens because worktrees use .git as a file (pointing to the main repo)
   501→// rather than a directory, and go-git needs to route paths correctly between
   502→// shared (.git/) and per-worktree (.git/worktrees/<name>/) locations.
   503→//
   504→// The function first uses 'git rev-parse --show-toplevel' to find the repository
   505→// root, which works correctly even when called from a subdirectory within the repo.
   506→func OpenRepository() (*git.Repository, error) {
   507→	// First, find the repository root using git rev-parse --show-toplevel
   508→	// This works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository
   509→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   510→	if err != nil {
   511→		// Fallback to current directory if git command fails
   512→		// (e.g., if git is not installed or we're not in a repo)
   513→		repoRoot = "."
   514→	}
   515→
   516→	repo, err := git.PlainOpenWithOptions(repoRoot, &git.PlainOpenOptions{
   517→		EnableDotGitCommonDir: true,
   518→	})
   519→	if err != nil {
   520→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   521→	}
   522→	return repo, nil
   523→}
   524→
   525→// IsInsideWorktree returns true if the current directory is inside a git worktree
   526→// (as opposed to the main repository). Worktrees have .git as a file pointing
   527→// to the main repo, while the main repo has .git as a directory.
   528→// This function works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   529→func IsInsideWorktree() bool {
   530→	// First find the repository root
   531→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   532→	if err != nil {
   533→		return false
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	gitPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, gitDir)
   537→	gitInfo, err := os.Stat(gitPath)
   538→	if err != nil {
   539→		return false
   540→	}
   541→	return !gitInfo.IsDir()
   542→}
   543→
   544→// GetMainRepoRoot returns the root directory of the main repository.
   545→// In the main repo, this is the worktree path (repo root).
   546→// In a worktree, this parses the .git file to find the main repo.
   547→// This function works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   548→//
   549→// Per gitrepository-layout(5), a worktree's .git file is a "gitfile" containing
   550→// "gitdir: <path>" pointing to $GIT_DIR/worktrees/<id> in the main repository.
   551→// See: https://git-scm.com/docs/gitrepository-layout
   552→func GetMainRepoRoot() (string, error) {
   553→	// First find the worktree/repo root
   554→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   555→	if err != nil {
   556→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   557→	}
   558→
   559→	if !IsInsideWorktree() {
   560→		return repoRoot, nil
   561→	}
   562→
   563→	// Worktree .git file contains: "gitdir: /path/to/main/.git/worktrees/<id>"
   564→	gitFilePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, gitDir)
   565→	content, err := os.ReadFile(gitFilePath) //nolint:gosec // G304: gitFilePath is constructed from repo root, not user input
   566→	if err != nil {
   567→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read .git file: %w", err)
   568→	}
   569→
   570→	gitdir := strings.TrimSpace(string(content))
   571→	gitdir = strings.TrimPrefix(gitdir, "gitdir: ")
   572→
   573→	// Extract main repo root: everything before "/.git/"
   574→	idx := strings.LastIndex(gitdir, "/.git/")
   575→	if idx < 0 {
   576→		return "", fmt.Errorf("unexpected gitdir format: %s", gitdir)
   577→	}
   578→	return gitdir[:idx], nil
   579→}
   580→
   581→// GetGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   582→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   583→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   584→// Uses git rev-parse --git-common-dir for reliable handling of worktrees.
   585→func GetGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   586→	ctx := context.Background()
   587→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   588→	cmd.Dir = "."
   589→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   590→	if err != nil {
   591→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   595→
   596→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   597→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   598→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   599→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   600→	}
   601→
   602→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   603→}
   604→
   605→// GetWorktreePath returns the absolute path to the current worktree root.
   606→// This is the working directory path, not the git directory.
   607→func GetWorktreePath() (string, error) {
   608→	ctx := context.Background()
   609→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
   610→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   611→	if err != nil {
   612→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   613→	}
   614→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), nil
   615→}
   616→
   617→// EnsureEntireGitignore ensures all required entries are in .entire/.gitignore
   618→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   619→func EnsureEntireGitignore() error {
   620→	// Get absolute path for the gitignore file
   621→	gitignoreAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(entireGitignore)
   622→	if err != nil {
   623→		gitignoreAbs = entireGitignore // Fallback to relative
   624→	}
   625→
   626→	// Read existing content
   627→	var content string
   628→	if data, err := os.ReadFile(gitignoreAbs); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
   629→		content = string(data)
   630→	}
   631→
   632→	// All entries that should be in .entire/.gitignore
   633→	requiredEntries := []string{
   634→		"tmp/",
   635→		"settings.local.json",
   636→		"metadata/",
   637→		"logs/",
   638→	}
   639→
   640→	// Track what needs to be added
   641→	var toAdd []string
   642→	for _, entry := range requiredEntries {
   643→		if !strings.Contains(content, entry) {
   644→			toAdd = append(toAdd, entry)
   645→		}
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	// Nothing to add
   649→	if len(toAdd) == 0 {
   650→		return nil
   651→	}
   652→
   653→	// Ensure .entire directory exists
   654→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(gitignoreAbs), 0o750); err != nil {
   655→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   656→	}
   657→
   658→	// Append missing entries to gitignore
   659→	var sb strings.Builder
   660→	for _, entry := range toAdd {
   661→		sb.WriteString(entry + "\n")
   662→	}
   663→	content += sb.String()
   664→
   665→	if err := os.WriteFile(gitignoreAbs, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
   666→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write gitignore: %w", err)
   667→	}
   668→	return nil
   669→}
   670→
   671→// checkCanRewind checks if working directory is clean enough for rewind.
   672→// Returns (canRewind, reason, error). Shared by shadow and linear-shadow strategies.
   673→func checkCanRewind() (bool, string, error) {
   674→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   675→	if err != nil {
   676→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   677→	}
   678→
   679→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   680→	if err != nil {
   681→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   682→	}
   683→
   684→	status, err := worktree.Status()
   685→	if err != nil {
   686→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get status: %w", err)
   687→	}
   688→
   689→	if status.IsClean() {
   690→		return true, "", nil
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	var modified, added, deleted []string
   694→	for file, st := range status {
   695→		// Skip .entire directory
   696→		if paths.IsInfrastructurePath(file) {
   697→			continue
   698→		}
   699→
   700→		// Skip untracked files
   701→		if st.Worktree == git.Untracked {
   702→			continue
   703→		}
   704→
   705→		switch {
   706→		case st.Staging == git.Added || st.Worktree == git.Added:
   707→			added = append(added, file)
   708→		case st.Staging == git.Deleted || st.Worktree == git.Deleted:
   709→			deleted = append(deleted, file)
   710→		case st.Staging == git.Modified || st.Worktree == git.Modified:
   711→			modified = append(modified, file)
   712→		}
   713→	}
   714→
   715→	if len(modified) == 0 && len(added) == 0 && len(deleted) == 0 {
   716→		return true, "", nil
   717→	}
   718→
   719→	var msg strings.Builder
   720→	msg.WriteString("You have uncommitted changes:\n")
   721→	for _, f := range modified {
   722→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  modified:   %s\n", f))
   723→	}
   724→	for _, f := range added {
   725→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  added:      %s\n", f))
   726→	}
   727→	for _, f := range deleted {
   728→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  deleted:    %s\n", f))
   729→	}
   730→	msg.WriteString("\nPlease commit or stash your changes before rewinding.")
   731→
   732→	return false, msg.String(), nil
   733→}
   734→
   735→// checkCanRewindWithWarning checks working directory and returns a warning with diff stats.
   736→// Unlike checkCanRewind, this always returns canRewind=true but includes a warning message
   737→// with +/- line stats for uncommitted changes. Used by manual-commit strategy.
   738→func checkCanRewindWithWarning() (bool, string, error) {
   739→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   740→	if err != nil {
   741→		// Can't open repo - still allow rewind but without stats
   742→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if repo can't be opened
   743→	}
   744→
   745→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   746→	if err != nil {
   747→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if worktree can't be accessed
   748→	}
   749→
   750→	status, err := worktree.Status()
   751→	if err != nil {
   752→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if status can't be retrieved
   753→	}
   754→
   755→	if status.IsClean() {
   756→		return true, "", nil
   757→	}
   758→
   759→	// Get HEAD commit tree for comparison - if we can't get it, just return without stats
   760→	head, err := repo.Head()
   761→	if err != nil {
   762→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even without HEAD (e.g., empty repo)
   763→	}
   764→
   765→	headCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   766→	if err != nil {
   767→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if commit lookup fails
   768→	}
   769→
   770→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
   771→	if err != nil {
   772→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if tree lookup fails
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	type fileChange struct {
   776→		status   string // "modified", "added", "deleted"
   777→		added    int
   778→		removed  int
   779→		filename string
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	var changes []fileChange
   783→	// Use repo root, not cwd - git status returns paths relative to repo root
   784→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   785→	if err != nil {
   786→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if repo root lookup fails
   787→	}
   788→
   789→	for file, st := range status {
   790→		// Skip .entire directory
   791→		if paths.IsInfrastructurePath(file) {
   792→			continue
   793→		}
   794→
   795→		// Skip untracked files
   796→		if st.Worktree == git.Untracked {
   797→			continue
   798→		}
   799→
   800→		var change fileChange
   801→		change.filename = file
   802→
   803→		switch {
   804→		case st.Staging == git.Added || st.Worktree == git.Added:
   805→			change.status = "added"
   806→			// New file - count all lines as added
   807→			absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   808→			if content, err := os.ReadFile(absPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // absPath is repo root + relative path from git status
   809→				change.added = countLines(content)
   810→			}
   811→		case st.Staging == git.Deleted || st.Worktree == git.Deleted:
   812→			change.status = "deleted"
   813→			// Deleted file - count lines from HEAD as removed
   814→			if entry, err := headTree.File(file); err == nil {
   815→				if content, err := entry.Contents(); err == nil {
   816→					change.removed = countLines([]byte(content))
   817→				}
   818→			}
   819→		case st.Staging == git.Modified || st.Worktree == git.Modified:
   820→			change.status = "modified"
   821→			// Modified file - compute diff stats
   822→			var headContent, workContent []byte
   823→			if entry, err := headTree.File(file); err == nil {
   824→				if content, err := entry.Contents(); err == nil {
   825→					headContent = []byte(content)
   826→				}
   827→			}
   828→			absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   829→			if content, err := os.ReadFile(absPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // absPath is repo root + relative path from git status
   830→				workContent = content
   831→			}
   832→			if headContent != nil && workContent != nil {
   833→				change.added, change.removed = computeDiffStats(headContent, workContent)
   834→			}
   835→		default:
   836→			continue
   837→		}
   838→
   839→		changes = append(changes, change)
   840→	}
   841→
   842→	if len(changes) == 0 {
   843→		return true, "", nil
   844→	}
   845→
   846→	// Sort changes by filename for consistent output
   847→	sort.Slice(changes, func(i, j int) bool {
   848→		return changes[i].filename < changes[j].filename
   849→	})
   850→
   851→	var msg strings.Builder
   852→	msg.WriteString("The following uncommitted changes will be reverted:\n")
   853→
   854→	totalAdded, totalRemoved := 0, 0
   855→	for _, c := range changes {
   856→		totalAdded += c.added
   857→		totalRemoved += c.removed
   858→
   859→		var stats string
   860→		switch {
   861→		case c.added > 0 && c.removed > 0:
   862→			stats = fmt.Sprintf("+%d/-%d", c.added, c.removed)
   863→		case c.added > 0:
   864→			stats = fmt.Sprintf("+%d", c.added)
   865→		case c.removed > 0:
   866→			stats = fmt.Sprintf("-%d", c.removed)
   867→		}
   868→
   869→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  %-10s %s", c.status+":", c.filename))
   870→		if stats != "" {
   871→			msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(" (%s)", stats))
   872→		}
   873→		msg.WriteString("\n")
   874→	}
   875→
   876→	if totalAdded > 0 || totalRemoved > 0 {
   877→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("\nTotal: +%d/-%d lines\n", totalAdded, totalRemoved))
   878→	}
   879→
   880→	return true, msg.String(), nil
   881→}
   882→
   883→// countLines counts the number of lines in content.
   884→func countLines(content []byte) int {
   885→	if len(content) == 0 {
   886→		return 0
   887→	}
   888→	count := 1
   889→	for _, b := range content {
   890→		if b == '\n' {
   891→			count++
   892→		}
   893→	}
   894→	// Don't count trailing newline as extra line
   895→	if len(content) > 0 && content[len(content)-1] == '\n' {
   896→		count--
   897→	}
   898→	return count
   899→}
   900→
   901→// computeDiffStats computes added and removed line counts between old and new content.
   902→// Uses a simple line-based diff algorithm.
   903→func computeDiffStats(oldContent, newContent []byte) (added, removed int) {
   904→	oldLines := splitLines(oldContent)
   905→	newLines := splitLines(newContent)
   906→
   907→	// Build a set of old lines with counts
   908→	oldSet := make(map[string]int)
   909→	for _, line := range oldLines {
   910→		oldSet[line]++
   911→	}
   912→
   913→	// Check which new lines are truly new
   914→	for _, line := range newLines {
   915→		if oldSet[line] > 0 {
   916→			oldSet[line]--
   917→		} else {
   918→			added++
   919→		}
   920→	}
   921→
   922→	// Remaining old lines are removed
   923→	for _, count := range oldSet {
   924→		removed += count
   925→	}
   926→
   927→	return added, removed
   928→}
   929→
   930→// splitLines splits content into lines, preserving empty lines.
   931→func splitLines(content []byte) []string {
   932→	if len(content) == 0 {
   933→		return nil
   934→	}
   935→	s := string(content)
   936→	// Remove trailing newline to avoid empty last element
   937→	s = strings.TrimSuffix(s, "\n")
   938→	return strings.Split(s, "\n")
   939→}
   940→
   941→// fileExists checks if a file exists at the given path.
   942→func fileExists(path string) bool {
   943→	_, err := os.Stat(path)
   944→	return err == nil
   945→}
   946→
   947→// StageFilesContext describes what type of staging operation this is (for messages).
   948→type StageFilesContext string
   949→
   950→const (
   951→	// StageForSession is used when staging files for a session checkpoint.
   952→	StageForSession StageFilesContext = "session"
   953→	// StageForTask is used when staging files for a task checkpoint.
   954→	StageForTask StageFilesContext = "task"
   955→)
   956→
   957→// StageFiles stages modified, new, and deleted files to the git worktree.
   958→//
   959→// This function handles three categories of file changes:
   960→//  1. Modified files: existing files that have been changed
   961→//  2. New files: files that were created during the session
   962→//  3. Deleted files: files that were removed during the session
   963→//
   964→// Error Handling Strategy:
   965→//   - Individual file staging errors are logged to stderr but don't fail the operation
   966→//   - This ensures that partial staging succeeds even if some files have issues
   967→//   - If a modified file no longer exists, it's treated as a deletion
   968→//
   969→// The stageCtx parameter is used for user-facing messages to indicate whether
   970→// this is staging for a session checkpoint or a task checkpoint.
   971→func StageFiles(worktree *git.Worktree, modified, newFiles, deleted []string, stageCtx StageFilesContext) {
   972→	// Get repo root for resolving file paths
   973→	// This is critical because fileExists() uses os.Stat() which resolves relative to CWD,
   974→	// but worktree.Add/Remove resolve relative to repo root. If CWD != repo root,
   975→	// fileExists() could return false for existing files, causing worktree.Remove()
   976→	// to incorrectly delete them.
   977→	repoRoot := worktree.Filesystem.Root()
   978→
   979→	// Stage modified files
   980→	for _, file := range modified {
   981→		// Resolve path relative to repo root for existence check
   982→		absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   983→		if fileExists(absPath) {
   984→			if _, err := worktree.Add(file); err != nil {
   985→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Failed to stage %s: %v\n", file, err)
   986→			} else {
   987→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Staged: %s\n", file)
   988→			}
   989→		} else {
   990→			// File was deleted - stage the deletion
   991→			if _, err := worktree.Remove(file); err != nil {
   992→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  File not found or deleted: %s\n", file)
   993→			}
   994→		}
   995→	}
   996→
   997→	// Stage new files
   998→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   999→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Staging %d new files created during %s:\n", len(newFiles), stageCtx)
  1000→		for _, file := range newFiles {
  1001→			if _, err := worktree.Add(file); err != nil {
  1002→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Failed to stage %s: %v\n", file, err)
  1003→			} else {
  1004→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Staged new file: %s\n", file)
  1005→			}
  1006→		}
  1007→	}
  1008→
  1009→	// Stage deleted files
  1010→	for _, file := range deleted {
  1011→		if _, err := worktree.Remove(file); err != nil {
  1012→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Failed to stage deleted file %s: %v\n", file, err)
  1013→		} else {
  1014→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Staged deleted file: %s\n", file)
  1015→		}
  1016→	}
  1017→}
  1018→
  1019→// getTaskCheckpointFromTree retrieves a task checkpoint from a commit tree.
  1020→// Shared implementation for shadow and linear-shadow strategies.
  1021→func getTaskCheckpointFromTree(point RewindPoint) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
  1022→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
  1023→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
  1024→	}
  1025→
  1026→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1027→	if err != nil {
  1028→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
  1029→	}
  1030→
  1031→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
  1032→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
  1033→	if err != nil {
  1034→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
  1035→	}
  1036→
  1037→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1038→	if err != nil {
  1039→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
  1040→	}
  1041→
  1042→	// Read checkpoint.json from the tree
  1043→	checkpointPath := point.MetadataDir + "/checkpoint.json"
  1044→	file, err := tree.File(checkpointPath)
  1045→	if err != nil {
  1046→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find checkpoint at %s: %w", checkpointPath, err)
  1047→	}
  1048→
  1049→	content, err := file.Contents()
  1050→	if err != nil {
  1051→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
  1052→	}
  1053→
  1054→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
  1055→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &checkpoint); err != nil {
  1056→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse checkpoint: %w", err)
  1057→	}
  1058→
  1059→	return &checkpoint, nil
  1060→}
  1061→
  1062→// getTaskTranscriptFromTree retrieves a task transcript from a commit tree.
  1063→// Shared implementation for shadow and linear-shadow strategies.
  1064→func getTaskTranscriptFromTree(point RewindPoint) ([]byte, error) {
  1065→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
  1066→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
  1067→	}
  1068→
  1069→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1070→	if err != nil {
  1071→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
  1072→	}
  1073→
  1074→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
  1075→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
  1076→	if err != nil {
  1077→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
  1078→	}
  1079→
  1080→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1081→	if err != nil {
  1082→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
  1083→	}
  1084→
  1085→	// MetadataDir format: .entire/metadata/<session>/tasks/<toolUseID>
  1086→	// Session transcript is at: .entire/metadata/<session>/<TranscriptFileName>
  1087→	sessionDir := filepath.Dir(filepath.Dir(point.MetadataDir))
  1088→
  1089→	// Try current format first, then legacy
  1090→	transcriptPath := sessionDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName
  1091→	file, err := tree.File(transcriptPath)
  1092→	if err != nil {
  1093→		transcriptPath = sessionDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy
  1094→		file, err = tree.File(transcriptPath)
  1095→		if err != nil {
  1096→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find transcript: %w", err)
  1097→		}
  1098→	}
  1099→
  1100→	content, err := file.Contents()
  1101→	if err != nil {
  1102→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
  1103→	}
  1104→
  1105→	return []byte(content), nil
  1106→}
  1107→
  1108→// HardResetWithProtection performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit.
  1109→// Uses the git CLI instead of go-git because go-git's HardReset incorrectly
  1110→// deletes untracked directories (like .entire/) even when they're in .gitignore.
  1111→// Returns the short commit ID (7 chars) on success for display purposes.
  1112→func HardResetWithProtection(commitHash plumbing.Hash) (shortID string, err error) {
  1113→	ctx := context.Background()
  1114→	hashStr := commitHash.String()
  1115→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "reset", "--hard", hashStr) //nolint:gosec // hashStr is a plumbing.Hash, not user input
  1116→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
  1117→		return "", fmt.Errorf("reset failed: %s: %w", strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
  1118→	}
  1119→
  1120→	// Return short commit ID for display
  1121→	shortID = hashStr
  1122→	if len(shortID) > 7 {
  1123→		shortID = shortID[:7]
  1124→	}
  1125→	return shortID, nil
  1126→}
  1127→
  1128→// collectUntrackedFiles collects all untracked files in the working directory.
  1129→// This is used to capture the initial state when starting a session,
  1130→// ensuring untracked files present at session start are preserved during rewind.
  1131→// Excludes .git/, .entire/, and .claude/ directories (which are system/config directories).
  1132→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
  1133→func collectUntrackedFiles() ([]string, error) {
  1134→	// Get repository root to walk from there
  1135→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
  1136→	if err != nil {
  1137→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to current directory
  1138→	}
  1139→
  1140→	var untrackedFiles []string
  1141→	err = filepath.Walk(repoRoot, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, walkErr error) error {
  1142→		if walkErr != nil {
  1143→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip filesystem errors during walk
  1144→		}
  1145→
  1146→		// Get path relative to repo root
  1147→		relPath, err := filepath.Rel(repoRoot, path)
  1148→		if err != nil {
  1149→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip paths we can't make relative
  1150→		}
  1151→
  1152→		// Skip directories
  1153→		if info.IsDir() {
  1154→			// Skip .git, .claude, and .entire directories
  1155→			if relPath == gitDir || relPath == claudeDir || relPath == entireDir ||
  1156→				strings.HasPrefix(relPath, gitDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, claudeDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, entireDir+"/") {
  1157→				return filepath.SkipDir
  1158→			}
  1159→			return nil
  1160→		}
  1161→
  1162→		// Skip files in special directories (shouldn't reach here due to SkipDir, but safety check)
  1163→		if strings.HasPrefix(relPath, gitDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, claudeDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, entireDir+"/") {
  1164→			return nil
  1165→		}
  1166→
  1167→		untrackedFiles = append(untrackedFiles, relPath)
  1168→		return nil
  1169→	})
  1170→	if err != nil {
  1171→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to walk directory: %w", err)
  1172→	}
  1173→
  1174→	return untrackedFiles, nil
  1175→}
  1176→
  1177→// ExtractSessionIDFromCommit extracts the session ID from a commit's trailers.
  1178→// It checks the Entire-Session trailer first, then falls back to extracting from
  1179→// the metadata directory path in the Entire-Metadata trailer.
  1180→// Returns empty string if no session ID is found.
  1181→func ExtractSessionIDFromCommit(commit *object.Commit) string {
  1182→	// Try Entire-Session trailer first
  1183→	if sessionID, found := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message); found {
  1184→		return sessionID
  1185→	}
  1186→
  1187→	// Try extracting from metadata directory (last path component)
  1188→	if metadataDir, found := trailers.ParseMetadata(commit.Message); found {
  1189→		return filepath.Base(metadataDir)
  1190→	}
  1191→
  1192→	return ""
  1193→}
  1194→
  1195→// NOTE: The following git tree helper functions have been moved to checkpoint/ package:
  1196→// - FlattenTree -> checkpoint.FlattenTree
  1197→// - CreateBlobFromContent -> checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent
  1198→// - BuildTreeFromEntries -> checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries
  1199→// - sortTreeEntries (internal to checkpoint package)
  1200→// - treeNode, insertIntoTree, buildTreeObject (internal to checkpoint package)
  1201→//
  1202→// See push_common.go and session_test.go for usage examples.
  1203→
  1204→// createCommit creates a commit object
  1205→func createCommit(repo *git.Repository, treeHash, parentHash plumbing.Hash, message, authorName, authorEmail string) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
  1206→	now := time.Now()
  1207→	sig := object.Signature{
  1208→		Name:  authorName,
  1209→		Email: authorEmail,
  1210→		When:  now,
  1211→	}
  1212→
  1213→	commit := &object.Commit{
  1214→		TreeHash:  treeHash,
  1215→		Author:    sig,
  1216→		Committer: sig,
  1217→		Message:   message,
  1218→	}
  1219→
  1220→	// Add parent if not a new branch
  1221→	if parentHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
  1222→		commit.ParentHashes = []plumbing.Hash{parentHash}
  1223→	}
  1224→
  1225→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
  1226→	if err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {
  1227→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to encode commit: %w", err)
  1228→	}
  1229→
  1230→	hash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
  1231→	if err != nil {
  1232→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to store commit: %w", err)
  1233→	}
  1234→
  1235→	return hash, nil
  1236→}
  1237→
  1238→// getSessionDescriptionFromTree reads the first line of prompt.txt or context.md from a git tree.
  1239→// This is the tree-based equivalent of getSessionDescription (which reads from filesystem).
  1240→//
  1241→// If metadataDir is provided, looks for files at metadataDir/prompt.txt or metadataDir/context.md.
  1242→// If metadataDir is empty, first tries the root of the tree (for when the tree is already
  1243→// the session directory, e.g., auto-commit strategy's sharded metadata), then falls back to
  1244→// searching for .entire/metadata/*/prompt.txt or context.md (for full worktree trees).
  1245→func getSessionDescriptionFromTree(tree *object.Tree, metadataDir string) string {
  1246→	// Helper to read first line from a file in tree
  1247→	readFirstLine := func(path string) string {
  1248→		file, err := tree.File(path)
  1249→		if err != nil {
  1250→			return ""
  1251→		}
  1252→		content, err := file.Contents()
  1253→		if err != nil {
  1254→			return ""
  1255→		}
  1256→		lines := strings.SplitN(content, "\n", 2)
  1257→		if len(lines) > 0 && lines[0] != "" {
  1258→			desc := strings.TrimSpace(lines[0])
  1259→			// Remove markdown header prefix if present
  1260→			return strings.TrimPrefix(desc, "# ")
  1261→		}
  1262→		return ""
  1263→	}
  1264→
  1265→	// If metadataDir is provided, look there directly
  1266→	if metadataDir != "" {
  1267→		if desc := readFirstLine(metadataDir + "/" + paths.PromptFileName); desc != "" {
  1268→			return desc
  1269→		}
  1270→		if desc := readFirstLine(metadataDir + "/" + paths.ContextFileName); desc != "" {
  1271→			return desc
  1272→		}
  1273→		return NoDescription
  1274→	}
  1275→
  1276→	// No metadataDir provided - first try looking at the root of the tree
  1277→	// (used when the tree is already the session directory)
  1278→	if desc := readFirstLine(paths.PromptFileName); desc != "" {
  1279→		return desc
  1280→	}
  1281→	if desc := readFirstLine(paths.ContextFileName); desc != "" {
  1282→		return desc
  1283→	}
  1284→
  1285→	// Fall back to searching for .entire/metadata/*/prompt.txt or context.md
  1286→	// (used when the tree is the full worktree)
  1287→	var desc string
  1288→	//nolint:errcheck // We ignore errors here as we're just searching for a description
  1289→	_ = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
  1290→		if desc != "" {
  1291→			return nil // Already found description
  1292→		}
  1293→		name := f.Name
  1294→		if strings.Contains(name, ".entire/metadata/") {
  1295→			if strings.HasSuffix(name, "/"+paths.PromptFileName) || strings.HasSuffix(name, "/"+paths.ContextFileName) {
  1296→				content, err := f.Contents()
  1297→				if err != nil {
  1298→					return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip files we can't read, continue searching
  1299→				}
  1300→				lines := strings.SplitN(content, "\n", 2)
  1301→				if len(lines) > 0 && lines[0] != "" {
  1302→					desc = strings.TrimSpace(lines[0])
  1303→					desc = strings.TrimPrefix(desc, "# ")
  1304→				}
  1305→			}
  1306→		}
  1307→		return nil
  1308→	})
  1309→
  1310→	if desc != "" {
  1311→		return desc
  1312→	}
  1313→	return NoDescription
  1314→}
  1315→
  1316→// GetGitAuthorFromRepo retrieves the git user.name and user.email from the repository config.
  1317→// It checks local config first, then falls back to global config.
  1318→// Returns ("Unknown", "unknown@local") if no user is configured - this allows
  1319→// operations to proceed even without git user config, which is especially useful
  1320→// for internal metadata commits on branches like entire/checkpoints/v1.
  1321→func GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo *git.Repository) (name, email string) {
  1322→	// Get repository config (includes local settings)
  1323→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
  1324→	if err == nil {
  1325→		name = cfg.User.Name
  1326→		email = cfg.User.Email
  1327→	}
  1328→
  1329→	// If not found in local config, try global config
  1330→	if name == "" || email == "" {
  1331→		globalCfg, err := config.LoadConfig(config.GlobalScope)
  1332→		if err == nil {
  1333→			if name == "" {
  1334→				name = globalCfg.User.Name
  1335→			}
  1336→			if email == "" {
  1337→				email = globalCfg.User.Email
  1338→			}
  1339→		}
  1340→	}
  1341→
  1342→	// Provide sensible defaults if git user is not configured
  1343→	if name == "" {
  1344→		name = "Unknown"
  1345→	}
  1346→	if email == "" {
  1347→		email = "unknown@local"
  1348→	}
  1349→
  1350→	return name, email
  1351→}
  1352→
  1353→// GetCurrentBranchName returns the short name of the current branch if HEAD points to a branch.
  1354→// Returns an empty string if in detached HEAD state or if there's an error reading HEAD.
  1355→// This is used to capture branch metadata for checkpoints.
  1356→func GetCurrentBranchName(repo *git.Repository) string {
  1357→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1358→	if err != nil || !head.Name().IsBranch() {
  1359→		return ""
  1360→	}
  1361→	return head.Name().Short()
  1362→}
  1363→
  1364→// getMainBranchHash returns the hash of the main branch (main or master).
  1365→// Returns ZeroHash if no main branch is found.
  1366→func GetMainBranchHash(repo *git.Repository) plumbing.Hash {
  1367→	// Try common main branch names
  1368→	for _, branchName := range []string{branchMain, branchMaster} {
  1369→		// Try local branch first
  1370→		ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
  1371→		if err == nil {
  1372→			return ref.Hash()
  1373→		}
  1374→		// Try remote tracking branch
  1375→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchName), true)
  1376→		if err == nil {
  1377→			return ref.Hash()
  1378→		}
  1379→	}
  1380→	return plumbing.ZeroHash
  1381→}
  1382→
  1383→// GetDefaultBranchName returns the name of the default branch.
  1384→// First checks origin/HEAD, then falls back to checking if main/master exists.
  1385→// Returns empty string if unable to determine.
  1386→// NOTE: Duplicated from cli/git_operations.go - see ENT-129 for consolidation.
  1387→func GetDefaultBranchName(repo *git.Repository) string {
  1388→	// Try to get the symbolic reference for origin/HEAD
  1389→	// Use resolved=false to get the symbolic ref itself, then extract its target
  1390→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", "HEAD"), false)
  1391→	if err == nil && ref != nil && ref.Type() == plumbing.SymbolicReference {
  1392→		target := ref.Target().String()
  1393→		if branchName, found := strings.CutPrefix(target, "refs/remotes/origin/"); found {
  1394→			return branchName
  1395→		}
  1396→	}
  1397→
  1398→	// Fallback: check if origin/main or origin/master exists
  1399→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchMain), true); err == nil {
  1400→		return branchMain
  1401→	}
  1402→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchMaster), true); err == nil {
  1403→		return branchMaster
  1404→	}
  1405→
  1406→	// Final fallback: check local branches
  1407→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchMain), true); err == nil {
  1408→		return branchMain
  1409→	}
  1410→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchMaster), true); err == nil {
  1411→		return branchMaster
  1412→	}
  1413→
  1414→	return ""
  1415→}
  1416→
  1417→// IsOnDefaultBranch checks if the repository HEAD is on the default branch.
  1418→// Returns (isOnDefault, currentBranchName).
  1419→// NOTE: Duplicated from cli/git_operations.go - see ENT-129 for consolidation.
  1420→func IsOnDefaultBranch(repo *git.Repository) (bool, string) {
  1421→	currentBranch := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
  1422→	if currentBranch == "" {
  1423→		return false, ""
  1424→	}
  1425→
  1426→	defaultBranch := GetDefaultBranchName(repo)
  1427→	if defaultBranch == "" {
  1428→		// Can't determine default, check common names
  1429→		if currentBranch == branchMain || currentBranch == branchMaster {
  1430→			return true, currentBranch
  1431→		}
  1432→		return false, currentBranch
  1433→	}
  1434→
  1435→	return currentBranch == defaultBranch, currentBranch
  1436→}
  1437→
```

```
    17→
    18→// Reset deletes the shadow branch and session state for the current HEAD.
    19→// This allows starting fresh without existing checkpoints.
    20→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) Reset() error {
    21→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	// Get current HEAD
    27→	head, err := repo.Head()
    28→	if err != nil {
    29→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    30→	}
    31→
    32→	// Get current worktree ID for shadow branch naming
    33→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
    38→	if err != nil {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree ID: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→
    42→	// Get shadow branch name for current HEAD
    43→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(head.Hash().String(), worktreeID)
    44→
    45→	// Check if shadow branch exists
    46→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
    47→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
    48→	hasShadowBranch := err == nil
    49→
    50→	// Find sessions for this commit
    51→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForCommit(head.Hash().String())
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		sessions = nil // Ignore error, treat as no sessions
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	// If nothing to reset, return early
    57→	if !hasShadowBranch && len(sessions) == 0 {
    58→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Nothing to reset for %s\n", shadowBranchName)
    59→		return nil
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	// Clear all sessions for this commit
    63→	clearedSessions := make([]string, 0)
    64→	for _, state := range sessions {
    65→		if err := s.clearSessionState(state.SessionID); err != nil {
    66→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to clear session state for %s: %v\n", state.SessionID, err)
    67→		} else {
    68→			clearedSessions = append(clearedSessions, state.SessionID)
    69→		}
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Report cleared session states with session IDs
    73→	if len(clearedSessions) > 0 {
    74→		for _, sessionID := range clearedSessions {
    75→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Cleared session state for %s\n", sessionID)
    76→		}
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// Delete the shadow branch if it exists
    80→	if hasShadowBranch {
    81→		if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(ref.Name()); err != nil {
    82→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete shadow branch: %w", err)
    83→		}
    84→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Deleted shadow branch %s\n", shadowBranchName)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	return nil
    88→}
    89→
    90→// ResetSession clears a single session's state and removes the shadow branch
    91→// if no other sessions reference it. File changes remain in the working directory.
    92→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) ResetSession(sessionID string) error {
    93→	// Load the session state
    94→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    95→	if err != nil {
    96→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
    97→	}
    98→	if state == nil {
    99→		return fmt.Errorf("session not found: %s", sessionID)
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	// Clear the session state file
   103→	if err := s.clearSessionState(sessionID); err != nil {
   104→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   105→	}
   106→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Cleared session state for %s\n", sessionID)
   107→
   108→	// Determine the shadow branch for this session
   109→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   110→
   111→	// Open repository
   112→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   113→	if err != nil {
   114→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	// Clean up shadow branch if no other sessions need it
   118→	if err := s.cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo, shadowBranchName, sessionID); err != nil {
   119→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to clean up shadow branch %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
   120→	} else {
   121→		// Check if it was actually deleted (branch no longer exists)
   122→		refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   123→		if _, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true); refErr != nil {
   124→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Deleted shadow branch %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   125→		}
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	return nil
   129→}
```

```
     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"os"
     6→
     7→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5"
    10→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded checks if HEAD has changed since the session started
    14→// and migrates the shadow branch to the new base commit if needed.
    15→//
    16→// This handles the scenario where Claude performs a rebase, pull, or other git operation
    17→// that changes HEAD mid-session (via a tool call), without a new prompt being submitted.
    18→// Without this migration, checkpoints would be saved to an orphaned shadow branch.
    19→//
    20→// Returns true if migration occurred, false otherwise.
    21→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
    22→	if state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
    23→		return false, nil
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	head, err := repo.Head()
    27→	if err != nil {
    28→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    29→	}
    30→
    31→	currentHead := head.Hash().String()
    32→	if state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
    33→		return false, nil // No migration needed
    34→	}
    35→
    36→	// HEAD changed - check if old shadow branch exists and migrate it
    37→	oldShadowBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    38→	newShadowBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(currentHead, state.WorktreeID)
    39→
    40→	// Guard against hash prefix collision: if both commits produce the same
    41→	// shadow branch name (same 7-char prefix), just update state - no ref rename needed
    42→	if oldShadowBranch == newShadowBranch {
    43→		state.BaseCommit = currentHead
    44→		return true, nil
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	oldRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(oldShadowBranch)
    48→	oldRef, err := repo.Reference(oldRefName, true)
    49→	if err != nil {
    50→		// Old shadow branch doesn't exist - just update state.BaseCommit
    51→		// This can happen if this is the first checkpoint after HEAD changed
    52→		state.BaseCommit = currentHead
    53→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session base commit to %s (HEAD changed during session)\n", currentHead[:7])
    54→		return true, nil //nolint:nilerr // err is "reference not found" which is fine - just need to update state
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Old shadow branch exists - move it to new base commit
    58→	newRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
    59→
    60→	// Create new reference pointing to same commit as old shadow branch
    61→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(newRefName, oldRef.Hash())
    62→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
    63→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create new shadow branch %s: %w", newShadowBranch, err)
    64→	}
    65→
    66→	// Delete old reference
    67→	if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(oldRefName); err != nil {
    68→		// Non-fatal: log but continue - the important thing is the new branch exists
    69→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to remove old shadow branch %s: %v\n", oldShadowBranch, err)
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Moved shadow branch from %s to %s (HEAD changed during session)\n",
    73→		oldShadowBranch, newShadowBranch)
    74→
    75→	// Update state with new base commit
    76→	state.BaseCommit = currentHead
    77→	return true, nil
    78→}
    79→
    80→// migrateAndPersistIfNeeded checks for HEAD changes, migrates the shadow branch if needed,
    81→// and persists the updated session state. Used by SaveChanges and SaveTaskCheckpoint.
    82→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) migrateAndPersistIfNeeded(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) error {
    83→	migrated, err := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, state)
    84→	if err != nil {
    85→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check/migrate shadow branch: %w", err)
    86→	}
    87→	if migrated {
    88→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
    89→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state after migration: %w", err)
    90→		}
    91→	}
    92→	return nil
    93→}
    94→
```

```
   276→// discardSession removes session state and cleans up the shadow branch.
   277→func discardSession(ss stuckSession, repo *git.Repository, errW io.Writer) error {
   278→	// Clear session state file
   279→	if err := strategy.ClearSessionState(ss.State.SessionID); err != nil {
   280→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   281→	}
   282→
   283→	// Delete shadow branch if it exists and no other sessions need it
   284→	if ss.HasShadowBranch {
   285→		if shouldDelete, err := canDeleteShadowBranch(ss.ShadowBranch, ss.State.SessionID); err != nil {
   286→			fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: could not check other sessions for shadow branch: %v\n", err)
   287→		} else if shouldDelete {
   288→			refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(ss.ShadowBranch)
   289→			ref, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   290→			if refErr == nil {
   291→				if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(ref.Name()); err != nil {
   292→					return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete shadow branch: %w", err)
   293→				}
   294→			}
   295→		}
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	return nil
   299→}
```

```
   604→// ShadowBranchExists checks if a shadow branch exists for the given base commit and worktree.
   605→// worktreeID should be empty for main worktree or the internal git worktree name for linked worktrees.
   606→func (s *GitStore) ShadowBranchExists(baseCommit, worktreeID string) bool {
   607→	shadowBranchName := ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   608→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   609→	_, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
   610→	return err == nil
   611→}
   612→
   613→// DeleteShadowBranch deletes the shadow branch for the given base commit and worktree.
   614→// worktreeID should be empty for main worktree or the internal git worktree name for linked worktrees.
   615→func (s *GitStore) DeleteShadowBranch(baseCommit, worktreeID string) error {
   616→	shadowBranchName := ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   617→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   618→	if err := s.repo.Storer.RemoveReference(refName); err != nil {
   619→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete shadow branch %s: %w", shadowBranchName, err)
   620→	}
   621→	return nil
   622→}
   623→
   624→// ShadowBranchNameForCommit returns the shadow branch name for a base commit hash
   625→// and worktree identifier. The worktree ID should be empty for the main worktree
   626→// or the internal git worktree name for linked worktrees.
   627→// Format: entire/<commit[:7]>-<hash(worktreeID)[:6]>
   628→func ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID string) string {
   629→	commitPart := baseCommit
   630→	if len(baseCommit) >= ShadowBranchHashLength {
   631→		commitPart = baseCommit[:ShadowBranchHashLength]
   632→	}
   633→	worktreeHash := HashWorktreeID(worktreeID)
   634→	return ShadowBranchPrefix + commitPart + "-" + worktreeHash
   635→}
   636→
```

```
    14→func TestIsShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
    15→	tests := []struct {
    16→		name       string
    17→		branchName string
    18→		want       bool
    19→	}{
    20→		// Valid shadow branches - old format (7+ hex chars)
    21→		{"old format: 7 hex chars", "entire/abc1234", true},
    22→		{"old format: 7 hex chars numeric", "entire/1234567", true},
    23→		{"old format: full commit hash", "entire/abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01", true},
    24→		{"old format: mixed case hex", "entire/AbCdEf1", true},
    25→
    26→		// Valid shadow branches - new format with worktree hash (7 hex + dash + 6 hex)
    27→		{"new format: standard", "entire/abc1234-e3b0c4", true},
    28→		{"new format: numeric worktree hash", "entire/1234567-123456", true},
    29→		{"new format: full commit with worktree", "entire/abcdef0123456789-fedcba", true},
    30→		{"new format: mixed case", "entire/AbCdEf1-AbCdEf", true},
    31→
    32→		// Invalid patterns
    33→		{"empty after prefix", "entire/", false},
    34→		{"too short commit (6 chars)", "entire/abc123", false},
    35→		{"too short commit (1 char)", "entire/a", false},
    36→		{"non-hex chars in commit", "entire/ghijklm", false},
    37→		{"sessions branch", paths.MetadataBranchName, false},
    38→		{"no prefix", "abc1234", false},
    39→		{"wrong prefix", "feature/abc1234", false},
    40→		{"main branch", "main", false},
    41→		{"master branch", "master", false},
    42→		{"empty string", "", false},
    43→		{"just entire", "entire", false},
    44→		{"entire with slash only", "entire/", false},
    45→		{"worktree hash too short (5 chars)", "entire/abc1234-e3b0c", false},
    46→		{"worktree hash too long (7 chars)", "entire/abc1234-e3b0c44", false},
    47→		{"non-hex in worktree hash", "entire/abc1234-ghijkl", false},
    48→		{"missing commit hash", "entire/-e3b0c4", false},
    49→	}

   237→func TestDeleteShadowBranches_NonExistent(t *testing.T) {
   238→	// Setup: create a temp git repo
   239→	dir := t.TempDir()
   240→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   241→	if err != nil {
   242→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	t.Chdir(dir)
   246→
   247→	// Create initial commit
   248→	emptyTreeHash := plumbing.NewHash("4b825dc642cb6eb9a060e54bf8d69288fbee4904")
   249→	commitHash, err := createCommit(repo, emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, "initial commit", "test", "test@test.com")
   250→	if err != nil {
   251→		t.Fatalf("failed to create initial commit: %v", err)
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	// Create HEAD reference
   255→	headRef := plumbing.NewSymbolicReference(plumbing.HEAD, plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("master"))
   256→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(headRef); err != nil {
   257→		t.Fatalf("failed to set HEAD: %v", err)
   258→	}
   259→	masterRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("master"), commitHash)
   260→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(masterRef); err != nil {
   261→		t.Fatalf("failed to set master: %v", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	// Try to delete non-existent branches
   265→	nonExistent := []string{"entire/doesnotexist"}
   266→	deleted, failed, err := DeleteShadowBranches(nonExistent)
   267→	if err != nil {
   268→		t.Fatalf("DeleteShadowBranches() error = %v", err)
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	// Should have one failed branch
   272→	if len(deleted) != 0 {
   273→		t.Errorf("DeleteShadowBranches() deleted %d branches, want 0", len(deleted))
   274→	}
   275→	if len(failed) != 1 {
   276→		t.Errorf("DeleteShadowBranches() failed %d branches, want 1", len(failed))
   277→	}
   278→}
   279→
   280→func TestDeleteShadowBranches_Empty(t *testing.T) {
   281→	// Setup: create a temp git repo
   282→	dir := t.TempDir()
   283→	_, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   284→	if err != nil {
   285→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   286→	}
   287→
   288→	t.Chdir(dir)
   289→
   290→	// Delete empty list should return empty results
   291→	deleted, failed, err := DeleteShadowBranches([]string{})
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		t.Fatalf("DeleteShadowBranches() error = %v", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	if len(deleted) != 0 || len(failed) != 0 {
   297→		t.Errorf("DeleteShadowBranches([]) = (%v, %v), want ([], [])", deleted, failed)
   298→	}
   299→}
   300→
   301→// TestListOrphanedSessionStates_RecentSessionNotOrphaned tests that recently started
   302→// sessions are NOT marked as orphaned, even if they have no checkpoints yet.
   303→//
   304→// P1 Bug: A session that just started (via InitializeSession) but hasn't created
   305→// its first checkpoint yet would be incorrectly marked as orphaned because it has:
   306→// - A session state file
   307→// - No checkpoints on entire/checkpoints/v1
   308→// - No shadow branch (if using auto-commit strategy, or before first checkpoint)
   309→//
   310→// This test should FAIL with the current implementation, demonstrating the bug.
   311→func TestListOrphanedSessionStates_RecentSessionNotOrphaned(t *testing.T) {
   312→	// Setup: create a temp git repo
   313→	dir := t.TempDir()
   314→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   315→	if err != nil {
   316→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	t.Chdir(dir)
   320→
   321→	// Create initial commit
   322→	emptyTreeHash := plumbing.NewHash("4b825dc642cb6eb9a060e54bf8d69288fbee4904")
   323→	commitHash, err := createCommit(repo, emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, "initial commit", "test", "test@test.com")
   324→	if err != nil {
   325→		t.Fatalf("failed to create initial commit: %v", err)
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Create HEAD reference
   329→	headRef := plumbing.NewSymbolicReference(plumbing.HEAD, plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("master"))
   330→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(headRef); err != nil {
   331→		t.Fatalf("failed to set HEAD: %v", err)
   332→	}
   333→	masterRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("master"), commitHash)
   334→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(masterRef); err != nil {
   335→		t.Fatalf("failed to set master: %v", err)
   336→	}
   337→
   338→	// Create a session state file that was JUST started (simulating InitializeSession)
   339→	// This session has no checkpoints and no shadow branch yet
   340→	state := &SessionState{
   341→		SessionID:  "recent-session-123",
   342→		BaseCommit: commitHash.String(), // Full 40-char hash
   343→		StartedAt:  time.Now(),          // Just started!
   344→		StepCount:  0,                   // No checkpoints yet
   345→	}
   346→	if err := SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   347→		t.Fatalf("SaveSessionState() error = %v", err)
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	// List orphaned session states
   351→	orphaned, err := ListOrphanedSessionStates()
   352→	if err != nil {
   353→		t.Fatalf("ListOrphanedSessionStates() error = %v", err)
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	// The recently started session should NOT be marked as orphaned
   357→	// because it's actively being used (StartedAt is recent)
   358→	for _, item := range orphaned {
   359→		if item.ID == "recent-session-123" {
   360→			t.Errorf("ListOrphanedSessionStates() incorrectly marked recent session as orphaned.\n"+
   361→				"Session was started %v ago, which is too recent to be considered orphaned.\n"+
   362→				"Expected: session to be protected from cleanup during active use.\n"+
   363→				"Got: session marked as orphaned with reason: %q",
   364→				time.Since(state.StartedAt), item.Reason)
   365→		}
   366→	}
   367→}
   368→
   369→// TestListOrphanedSessionStates_ShadowBranchMatching tests that session states are correctly
   370→// matched against shadow branches using worktree-specific naming.
   371→//
   372→// Shadow branches use the format "entire/<commit[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>" and session states
   373→// store both the full BaseCommit and WorktreeID. The comparison constructs the expected
   374→// branch name from these fields and checks if it exists.
   375→func TestListOrphanedSessionStates_ShadowBranchMatching(t *testing.T) {
   376→	// Setup: create a temp git repo
   377→	dir := t.TempDir()
   378→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   379→	if err != nil {
   380→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   381→	}
   382→
   383→	t.Chdir(dir)
   384→
   385→	// Create initial commit
   386→	emptyTreeHash := plumbing.NewHash("4b825dc642cb6eb9a060e54bf8d69288fbee4904")
   387→	commitHash, err := createCommit(repo, emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, "initial commit", "test", "test@test.com")
   388→	if err != nil {
   389→		t.Fatalf("failed to create initial commit: %v", err)
   390→	}
   391→
   392→	// Create HEAD reference
   393→	headRef := plumbing.NewSymbolicReference(plumbing.HEAD, plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("master"))
   394→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(headRef); err != nil {
   395→		t.Fatalf("failed to set HEAD: %v", err)
   396→	}
   397→	masterRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("master"), commitHash)
   398→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(masterRef); err != nil {
   399→		t.Fatalf("failed to set master: %v", err)
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	// Create a shadow branch using worktree-specific naming (matching real behavior)
   403→	// Real code: shadowBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   404→	fullHash := commitHash.String()
   405→	worktreeID := "" // Main worktree
   406→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(fullHash, worktreeID)
   407→	shadowRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName), commitHash)
   408→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(shadowRef); err != nil {
   409→		t.Fatalf("failed to create shadow branch: %v", err)
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	// Create a session state with the FULL 40-char hash and WorktreeID (matching real behavior)
   413→	// Real code: state.BaseCommit = head.Hash().String(), state.WorktreeID = worktreeID
   414→	state := &SessionState{
   415→		SessionID:  "session-with-shadow-branch",
   416→		BaseCommit: fullHash, // Full 40-char hash
   417→		WorktreeID: worktreeID,
   418→		StartedAt:  time.Now().Add(-1 * time.Hour),
   419→		StepCount:  1,
   420→	}
   421→	if err := SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   422→		t.Fatalf("SaveSessionState() error = %v", err)
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	// Verify the shadow branch exists with worktree-specific name
   426→	shadowBranches, err := ListShadowBranches()
   427→	if err != nil {
   428→		t.Fatalf("ListShadowBranches() error = %v", err)
   429→	}
   430→	if len(shadowBranches) != 1 || shadowBranches[0] != shadowBranchName {
   431→		t.Fatalf("Expected shadow branch %q, got %v", shadowBranchName, shadowBranches)
   432→	}
   433→
   434→	// Log info about the branch naming
   435→	t.Logf("Shadow branch name: %q", shadowBranchName)
   436→	t.Logf("Session BaseCommit (40 chars): %q", fullHash)
   437→	t.Logf("Session WorktreeID: %q", worktreeID)
   438→
   439→	// List orphaned session states
   440→	orphaned, err := ListOrphanedSessionStates()
   441→	if err != nil {
   442→		t.Fatalf("ListOrphanedSessionStates() error = %v", err)
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	// The session should NOT be marked as orphaned because it HAS a shadow branch!
   446→	// With worktree-specific naming, the expected branch name is constructed from
   447→	// BaseCommit and WorktreeID, which should match the actual shadow branch.
   448→	for _, item := range orphaned {
   449→		if item.ID == "session-with-shadow-branch" {
   450→			t.Errorf("ListOrphanedSessionStates() incorrectly marked session as orphaned.\n"+
   451→				"Shadow branch exists: %q\n"+
   452→				"Session BaseCommit: %q\n"+
   453→				"Session WorktreeID: %q\n"+
   454→				"Expected branch: %q\n"+
   455→				"Got: session marked as orphaned with reason: %q",
   456→				shadowBranchName, fullHash, worktreeID,
   457→				checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(fullHash, worktreeID), item.Reason)
   458→		}
   459→	}
   460→}
```

```
     1→// Package logging provides structured logging for the Entire CLI using slog.
     2→//
     3→// Usage:
     4→//
     5→//	// Initialize logger for a session (typically at session start)
     6→//	if err := logging.Init(sessionID); err != nil {
     7→//	    // handle error
     8→//	}
     9→//	defer logging.Close()
    10→//
    11→//	// Add context values
    12→//	ctx = logging.WithSession(ctx, sessionID)
    13→//	ctx = logging.WithToolCall(ctx, toolCallID)
    14→//
    15→//	// Log with context - session/tool IDs extracted automatically
    16→//	logging.Info(ctx, "hook invoked",
    17→//	    slog.String("hook", hookName),
    18→//	    slog.String("branch", branch),
    19→//	)
    20→package logging
    21→
    22→import (
    23→	"bufio"
    24→	"context"
    25→	"fmt"
    26→	"io"
    27→	"log/slog"
    28→	"os"
    29→	"path/filepath"
    30→	"strings"
    31→	"sync"
    32→	"time"
    33→
    34→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    35→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    36→)
    37→
    38→// LogLevelEnvVar is the environment variable that controls log level.
    39→const LogLevelEnvVar = "ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL"
    40→
    41→// LogsDir is the directory where log files are stored (relative to repo root).
    42→const LogsDir = ".entire/logs"
    43→
    44→var (
    45→	// logger is the package-level logger instance
    46→	logger *slog.Logger
    47→
    48→	// logFile holds the current log file handle for cleanup
    49→	logFile *os.File
    50→
    51→	// logBufWriter wraps logFile with buffered I/O for performance
    52→	logBufWriter *bufio.Writer
    53→
    54→	// currentSessionID stores the session ID from Init() to include in all logs
    55→	currentSessionID string
    56→
    57→	// mu protects logger, logFile, logBufWriter, and currentSessionID
    58→	mu sync.RWMutex
    59→
    60→	// logLevelGetter is an optional callback to get log level from settings.
    61→	// Set by SetLogLevelGetter before Init is called.
    62→	logLevelGetter func() string
    63→)
    64→
    65→// SetLogLevelGetter sets a callback function to get the log level from settings.
    66→// This allows the logging package to read settings without a circular dependency.
    67→// The callback is only used if ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL env var is not set.
    68→func SetLogLevelGetter(getter func() string) {
    69→	mu.Lock()
    70→	defer mu.Unlock()
    71→	logLevelGetter = getter
    72→}
    73→
    74→// Init initializes the logger for a session, writing JSON logs to
    75→// .entire/logs/entire.log.
    76→//
    77→// If sessionID is non-empty, it is stored as an slog attribute on every log line for filtering.
    78→// If the log file cannot be created, falls back to stderr.
    79→// Log level is controlled by ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL environment variable.
    80→func Init(sessionID string) error {
    81→	// Validate session ID if provided (used only for the slog attribute, not the filename)
    82→	if sessionID != "" {
    83→		if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
    84→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID for logging: %w", err)
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	mu.Lock()
    89→	defer mu.Unlock()
    90→
    91→	// Close any existing log file (flush buffer first)
    92→	if logBufWriter != nil {
    93→		_ = logBufWriter.Flush()
    94→		logBufWriter = nil
    95→	}
    96→	if logFile != nil {
    97→		_ = logFile.Close()
    98→		logFile = nil
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	// Get log level from environment first, then settings
   102→	levelStr := os.Getenv(LogLevelEnvVar)
   103→	if levelStr == "" && logLevelGetter != nil {
   104→		levelStr = logLevelGetter()
   105→	}
   106→	level := parseLogLevel(levelStr)
   107→
   108→	// Warn if invalid level was provided
   109→	if levelStr != "" && !isValidLogLevel(levelStr) {
   110→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: invalid log level %q, defaulting to INFO\n", levelStr)
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	// Determine log file path
   114→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		// Fall back to current directory
   117→		repoRoot = "."
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	logsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, LogsDir)
   121→	if err := os.MkdirAll(logsPath, 0o750); err != nil {
   122→		// Fall back to stderr
   123→		logger = createLogger(os.Stderr, level)
   124→		return nil
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	logFilePath := filepath.Join(logsPath, "entire.log")
   128→	f, err := os.OpenFile(logFilePath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600) //nolint:gosec // fixed filename, not user-controlled
   129→	if err != nil {
   130→		// Fall back to stderr
   131→		logger = createLogger(os.Stderr, level)
   132→		return nil
   133→	}
   134→
   135→	logFile = f
   136→	logBufWriter = bufio.NewWriterSize(f, 8192) // 8KB buffer for batched writes
   137→	logger = createLogger(logBufWriter, level)
   138→	currentSessionID = sessionID
   139→
   140→	return nil
   141→}
   142→
   143→// Close closes the log file if one is open.
   144→// Flushes any buffered data before closing.
   145→// Safe to call multiple times.
   146→func Close() {
   147→	mu.Lock()
   148→	defer mu.Unlock()
   149→
   150→	if logBufWriter != nil {
   151→		_ = logBufWriter.Flush()
   152→		logBufWriter = nil
   153→	}
   154→	if logFile != nil {
   155→		_ = logFile.Close()
   156→		logFile = nil
   157→	}
   158→	currentSessionID = ""
   159→}
   160→
   161→// resetLogger resets the logger to nil (for testing).
   162→func resetLogger() {
   163→	mu.Lock()
   164→	defer mu.Unlock()
   165→	logger = nil
   166→	currentSessionID = ""
   167→	if logBufWriter != nil {
   168→		_ = logBufWriter.Flush()
   169→		logBufWriter = nil
   170→	}
   171→	if logFile != nil {
   172→		_ = logFile.Close()
   173→		logFile = nil
   174→	}
   175→}
   176→
   177→// getLogger returns the current logger, or a default stderr logger if not initialized.
   178→func getLogger() *slog.Logger {
   179→	mu.RLock()
   180→	defer mu.RUnlock()
   181→
   182→	if logger == nil {
   183→		// Return default stderr logger
   184→		return slog.Default()
   185→	}
   186→	return logger
   187→}
   188→
   189→// getSessionID returns the current session ID (thread-safe).
   190→func getSessionID() string {
   191→	mu.RLock()
   192→	defer mu.RUnlock()
   193→	return currentSessionID
   194→}
   195→
   196→// createLogger creates a JSON logger writing to the given writer at the specified level.
   197→func createLogger(w io.Writer, level slog.Level) *slog.Logger {
   198→	opts := &slog.HandlerOptions{
   199→		Level: level,
   200→	}
   201→	handler := slog.NewJSONHandler(w, opts)
   202→	return slog.New(handler)
   203→}
   204→
   205→// parseLogLevel parses a log level string to slog.Level.
   206→// Returns slog.LevelInfo for empty or invalid values.
   207→func parseLogLevel(s string) slog.Level {
   208→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(s)) {
   209→	case "DEBUG":
   210→		return slog.LevelDebug
   211→	case "INFO":
   212→		return slog.LevelInfo
   213→	case "WARN", "WARNING":
   214→		return slog.LevelWarn
   215→	case "ERROR":
   216→		return slog.LevelError
   217→	default:
   218→		return slog.LevelInfo
   219→	}
   220→}
   221→
   222→// isValidLogLevel checks if the given string is a valid log level.
   223→func isValidLogLevel(s string) bool {
   224→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(s)) {
   225→	case "DEBUG", "INFO", "WARN", "WARNING", "ERROR", "":
   226→		return true
   227→	default:
   228→		return false
   229→	}
   230→}
   231→
   232→// Debug logs at DEBUG level with context values automatically extracted.
   233→func Debug(ctx context.Context, msg string, attrs ...any) {
   234→	log(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, msg, attrs...)
   235→}
   236→
   237→// Info logs at INFO level with context values automatically extracted.
   238→func Info(ctx context.Context, msg string, attrs ...any) {
   239→	log(ctx, slog.LevelInfo, msg, attrs...)
   240→}
   241→
   242→// Warn logs at WARN level with context values automatically extracted.
   243→func Warn(ctx context.Context, msg string, attrs ...any) {
   244→	log(ctx, slog.LevelWarn, msg, attrs...)
   245→}
   246→
   247→// Error logs at ERROR level with context values automatically extracted.
   248→func Error(ctx context.Context, msg string, attrs ...any) {
   249→	log(ctx, slog.LevelError, msg, attrs...)
   250→}
   251→
   252→// LogDuration logs a message with duration_ms calculated from the start time.
   253→// The level parameter specifies the log level (use slog.LevelDebug, slog.LevelInfo, etc).
   254→// Designed for use with defer:
   255→//
   256→//	defer logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelInfo, "operation completed", time.Now())
   257→//
   258→// Or with additional attrs:
   259→//
   260→//	defer logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, "hook executed", start,
   261→//	    slog.String("hook", hookName),
   262→//	    slog.Bool("success", true),
   263→//	)
   264→func LogDuration(ctx context.Context, level slog.Level, msg string, start time.Time, attrs ...any) {
   265→	durationMs := time.Since(start).Milliseconds()
   266→
   267→	// Prepend duration_ms to attrs
   268→	allAttrs := make([]any, 0, len(attrs)+1)
   269→	allAttrs = append(allAttrs, slog.Int64("duration_ms", durationMs))
   270→	allAttrs = append(allAttrs, attrs...)
   271→
   272→	log(ctx, level, msg, allAttrs...)
   273→}
   274→
   275→// log is the internal logging function that extracts context values and logs.
   276→func log(ctx context.Context, level slog.Level, msg string, attrs ...any) {
   277→	l := getLogger()
   278→
   279→	// Build attributes slice with session ID first (if set)
   280→	var allAttrs []any
   281→
   282→	// Add session ID from Init() if set (always first for consistency)
   283→	globalSessionID := getSessionID()
   284→	if globalSessionID != "" {
   285→		allAttrs = append(allAttrs, slog.String("session_id", globalSessionID))
   286→	}
   287→
   288→	// Extract context values, skipping session_id if already added from Init()
   289→	contextAttrs := attrsFromContext(ctx, globalSessionID)
   290→	for _, a := range contextAttrs {
   291→		allAttrs = append(allAttrs, a)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	// Add caller-provided attributes
   295→	allAttrs = append(allAttrs, attrs...)
   296→
   297→	// Pass nil context to slog as we've already extracted context values as attributes.
   298→	// slog handlers are expected to handle nil context gracefully.
   299→	l.Log(nil, level, msg, allAttrs...) //nolint:staticcheck // nil context is intentional - we extract values as attributes
   300→}
   301→
   302→// attrsFromContext extracts logging attributes from a context.
   303→// If globalSessionID is non-empty, skips adding session_id from context to avoid duplicates.
   304→func attrsFromContext(ctx context.Context, globalSessionID string) []slog.Attr {
   305→	if ctx == nil {
   306→		return nil
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	var attrs []slog.Attr
   310→
   311→	// Only add session_id from context if not already set globally
   312→	if globalSessionID == "" {
   313→		if v := ctx.Value(sessionIDKey); v != nil {
   314→			if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   315→				attrs = append(attrs, slog.String("session_id", s))
   316→			}
   317→		}
   318→	}
   319→	if v := ctx.Value(parentSessionIDKey); v != nil {
   320→		if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   321→			attrs = append(attrs, slog.String("parent_session_id", s))
   322→		}
   323→	}
   324→	if v := ctx.Value(toolCallIDKey); v != nil {
   325→		if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   326→			attrs = append(attrs, slog.String("tool_call_id", s))
   327→		}
   328→	}
   329→	if v := ctx.Value(componentKey); v != nil {
   330→		if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   331→			attrs = append(attrs, slog.String("component", s))
   332→		}
   333→	}
   334→	if v := ctx.Value(agentKey); v != nil {
   335→		if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   336→			attrs = append(attrs, slog.String("agent", s))
   337→		}
   338→	}
   339→
   340→	return attrs
   341→}
   342→
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go",
  "old_string": "// HardResetWithProtection performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit.",
  "new_string": "// DeleteBranchCLI deletes a git branch using the git CLI.\n// Uses `git branch -D` instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5\n// doesn't properly persist deletions when refs are packed (.git/packed-refs)\n// or in a worktree context. This is the same class of go-git v5 bug that\n// affects checkout and reset --hard (see HardResetWithProtection).\nfunc DeleteBranchCLI(branchName string) error {\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"branch\", \"-D\", branchName)\n\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to delete branch %s: %s: %w\", branchName, strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// HardResetWithProtection performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit."
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "// deleteShadowBranch deletes a shadow branch by name using an existing repo handle.\n// Returns nil if the branch doesn't exist (idempotent).\nfunc deleteShadowBranch(repo *git.Repository, branchName string) error {\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName)\n\n\t// Check if reference exists\n\tref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// Branch doesn't exist - nothing to delete (idempotent)\n\t\treturn nil //nolint:nilerr // Not an error condition - branch already gone\n\t}\n\n\t// Delete the reference\n\tif err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(ref.Name()); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to delete branch %s: %w\", branchName, err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// deleteShadowBranch deletes a shadow branch by name.\n// Returns nil if the branch doesn't exist (idempotent).\n// Uses git CLI instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5\n// doesn't properly persist deletions with packed refs or worktrees.\nfunc deleteShadowBranch(_ *git.Repository, branchName string) error {\n\terr := DeleteBranchCLI(branchName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// If the branch doesn't exist, git branch -D returns an error.\n\t\t// Treat this as idempotent - not an error condition.\n\t\tif strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"not found\") {\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}"
}
```

```
   594→// cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused deletes a shadow branch if no other active sessions reference it.
   595→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo *git.Repository, shadowBranchName, excludeSessionID string) error {
   596→	// List all session states to check if any other session uses this shadow branch
   597→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   598→	if err != nil {
   599→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   600→	}
   601→
   602→	for _, state := range allStates {
   603→		if state.SessionID == excludeSessionID {
   604→			continue
   605→		}
   606→		otherShadow := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   607→		if otherShadow == shadowBranchName && state.StepCount > 0 {
   608→			// Another session still needs this shadow branch
   609→			return nil
   610→		}
   611→	}

   618→			return nil
   619→		}
   620→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch: %w", err)
   621→	}
   622→	return nil
   623→}
```
