     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"os"
     6→
     7→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5"
    10→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded checks if HEAD has changed since the session started
    14→// and migrates the shadow branch to the new base commit if needed.
    15→//
    16→// This handles the scenario where Claude performs a rebase, pull, or other git operation
    17→// that changes HEAD mid-session (via a tool call), without a new prompt being submitted.
    18→// Without this migration, checkpoints would be saved to an orphaned shadow branch.
    19→//
    20→// Returns true if migration occurred, false otherwise.
    21→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
    22→	if state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
    23→		return false, nil
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	head, err := repo.Head()
    27→	if err != nil {
    28→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    29→	}
    30→
    31→	currentHead := head.Hash().String()
    32→	if state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
    33→		return false, nil // No migration needed
    34→	}
    35→
    36→	// HEAD changed - check if old shadow branch exists and migrate it
    37→	oldShadowBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    38→	newShadowBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(currentHead, state.WorktreeID)
    39→
    40→	// Guard against hash prefix collision: if both commits produce the same
    41→	// shadow branch name (same 7-char prefix), just update state - no ref rename needed
    42→	if oldShadowBranch == newShadowBranch {
    43→		state.BaseCommit = currentHead
    44→		return true, nil
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	oldRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(oldShadowBranch)
    48→	oldRef, err := repo.Reference(oldRefName, true)
    49→	if err != nil {
    50→		// Old shadow branch doesn't exist - just update state.BaseCommit
    51→		// This can happen if this is the first checkpoint after HEAD changed
    52→		state.BaseCommit = currentHead
    53→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session base commit to %s (HEAD changed during session)\n", currentHead[:7])
    54→		return true, nil //nolint:nilerr // err is "reference not found" which is fine - just need to update state
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Old shadow branch exists - move it to new base commit
    58→	newRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
    59→
    60→	// Create new reference pointing to same commit as old shadow branch
    61→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(newRefName, oldRef.Hash())
    62→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
    63→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create new shadow branch %s: %w", newShadowBranch, err)
    64→	}
    65→
    66→	// Delete old reference via CLI (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)
    67→	if err := DeleteBranchCLI(oldShadowBranch); err != nil {
    68→		// Non-fatal: log but continue - the important thing is the new branch exists
    69→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to remove old shadow branch %s: %v\n", oldShadowBranch, err)
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Moved shadow branch from %s to %s (HEAD changed during session)\n",
    73→		oldShadowBranch, newShadowBranch)
    74→
    75→	// Update state with new base commit
    76→	state.BaseCommit = currentHead
    77→	return true, nil
    78→}
    79→
    80→// migrateAndPersistIfNeeded checks for HEAD changes, migrates the shadow branch if needed,
    81→// and persists the updated session state. Used by SaveChanges and SaveTaskCheckpoint.
    82→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) migrateAndPersistIfNeeded(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) error {
    83→	migrated, err := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, state)
    84→	if err != nil {
    85→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check/migrate shadow branch: %w", err)
    86→	}
    87→	if migrated {
    88→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
    89→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state after migration: %w", err)
    90→		}
    91→	}
    92→	return nil
    93→}
    94→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

  "new_string": "// DeleteShadowBranch deletes the shadow branch for the given base commit and worktree.\n// worktreeID should be empty for main worktree or the internal git worktree name for linked worktrees.\n// Uses git CLI instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5 doesn't properly\n// persist deletions with packed refs or worktrees.\nfunc (s *GitStore) DeleteShadowBranch(baseCommit, worktreeID string) error {\n\tshadowBranchName := ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"branch\", \"-D\", shadowBranchName)\n\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to delete shadow branch %s: %s: %w\", shadowBranchName, strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}"
}

> AGENT

**Update the test** - The `TestDeleteShadowBranches` test uses `t.Chdir()` which means it can't use `t.Parallel()`. The test creates branches with go-git's `SetReference` (loose refs), where the old code worked fine. Now that we use `git branch -D`, we need proper `git init` with a commit so git CLI works. The test already does `git.PlainInit` and creates a commit. But `git branch -D` requires the branch to be a real branch, not just a ref — let me check if the test setup is compatible with the CLI approach.

The test creates refs with go-git, then calls `DeleteShadowBranches`. With git CLI (`git branch -D`), the git command needs to find these refs. Since `git.PlainInit` creates a standard repo and `SetReference` writes loose refs, `git branch -D` should work fine. But we also need to verify the test verifies deletion correctly. Currently it uses `repo.Reference()` which reads from go-git — that should still reflect the deletion since the CLI actually removed the ref file.

Actually, the key issue is the test verifies using `repo.Reference()` — after deleting via CLI, go-git may have stale cached references. Let me update the test to verify via `git branch --list` CLI instead.

> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary of the changes:

## Changes Made

### Bug 1: Logging to stdio
- **`cmd/entire/cli/clean.go`** — Added `logging.Init("")` + `defer logging.Close()` at the start of `runClean()` so structured logs from `DeleteAllCleanupItems` go to `.entire/logs/entire.log` instead of falling back to stderr via `slog.Default()`.

### Bug 2: Shadow branches not deleted  
- **`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go`** — Added `DeleteBranchCLI(branchName string) error` helper that runs `git branch -D` via CLI, matching the pattern established by `HardResetWithProtection()`.

- **7 call sites updated** to use CLI-based deletion instead of go-git's `RemoveReference`:

  | File | Function | Change |
  |------|----------|--------|
  | `strategy/cleanup.go` | `DeleteShadowBranches()` | Replaced `RemoveReference` loop with `DeleteBranchCLI`; removed unused `OpenRepository()` call |
  | `strategy/manual_commit_git.go` | `deleteShadowBranch()` | Replaced with `DeleteBranchCLI`, idempotent on "not found" |
  | `strategy/manual_commit_reset.go` | `Reset()` | Replaced `RemoveReference` with `DeleteBranchCLI` |
  | `strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` | `cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused()` | Replaced with `DeleteBranchCLI`, idempotent on "not found" |
  | `strategy/manual_commit_migration.go` | `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` | Replaced old ref delete with `DeleteBranchCLI` |
  | `cli/sessions_fix.go` | `discardSession()` | Replaced with `strategy.DeleteBranchCLI` |
  | `checkpoint/temporary.go` | `GitStore.DeleteShadowBranch()` | Replaced with `exec.CommandContext` (can't import `strategy`) |

- **`strategy/clean_test.go`** — Updated `TestDeleteShadowBranches` verification to use `git branch --list` CLI instead of go-git's `repo.Reference()` to match the real code path.

+// DeleteBranchCLI deletes a git branch using the git CLI.
+// Uses `git branch -D` instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5
+// doesn't properly persist deletions when refs are packed (.git/packed-refs)
+// or in a worktree context. This is the same class of go-git v5 bug that
+// affects checkout and reset --hard (see HardResetWithProtection).
+func DeleteBranchCLI(branchName string) error {
+	ctx := context.Background()
+	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "branch", "-D", branchName)
+	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete branch %s: %s: %w", branchName, strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // HardResetWithProtection performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit.
 // Uses the git CLI instead of go-git because go-git's HardReset incorrectly
 // deletes untracked directories (like .entire/) even when they're in .gitignore.

@@ -610,15 +610,14 @@ func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo *git.Repository,
 		}
 	}
 
-	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch

-	ref, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
-	if refErr != nil {
-		// Branch already gone, nothing to clean up
-		return nil //nolint:nilerr // intentional: missing branch is not an error
-	}
-	if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(ref.Name()); err != nil {
-		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch reference: %w", err)
+	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch via CLI
+	// (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)

+		if strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not found") {

diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/mid_session_commit_test.go b/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/mid_session_commit_test.go
index 5d3c97f3..65437952 100644
--- a/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/mid_session_commit_test.go
+++ b/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/mid_session_commit_test.go
@@ -181,3 +181,32 @@ func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerForUnrelatedFile(t *testing.T)
 		t.Log("Correctly omitted checkpoint trailer for unrelated file commit")
 	}
 }
+
+// TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer tests that when an agent commits
+// (ACTIVE session + no TTY), the trailer is always added regardless of content
+// detection. This is the fast path that bypasses transcript analysis.
+func TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
+
+	session := env.NewSession()
+
+	// Start session (sets phase to ACTIVE)
+	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Create a file and commit as agent (no TTY)
+	env.WriteFile("agent_file.txt", "created by agent")
+	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent("Agent commit", "agent_file.txt")
+
+	// Agent commits should ALWAYS get a trailer (fast path, no content detection)
+	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
+	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
+	if checkpointID == "" {
+		t.Error("Agent commit during ACTIVE session should always get a checkpoint trailer")
+	} else {
+		t.Logf("Agent commit correctly got checkpoint trailer: %s", checkpointID)
+	}
+}

diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go b/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go

 	return []byte(content), nil
 }
 
+// DeleteBranchCLI deletes a git branch using the git CLI.
+// Uses `git branch -D` instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5
+// doesn't properly persist deletions when refs are packed (.git/packed-refs)
+// or in a worktree context. This is the same class of go-git v5 bug that
+// affects checkout and reset --hard (see HardResetWithProtection).
+func DeleteBranchCLI(branchName string) error {
+	ctx := context.Background()
+	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "branch", "-D", branchName)
+	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete branch %s: %s: %w", branchName, strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // HardResetWithProtection performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit.
 // Uses the git CLI instead of go-git because go-git's HardReset incorrectly
 // deletes untracked directories (like .entire/) even when they're in .gitignore.

-	}
-	if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(ref.Name()); err != nil {
-		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch reference: %w", err)
+	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch via CLI
+	// (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)
+	if err := DeleteBranchCLI(shadowBranchName); err != nil {
+		// Branch already gone is not an error
+		if strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not found") {
+			return nil

+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch: %w", err)

diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go b/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go

@@ -25,11 +25,36 @@ import (
 	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/utils/binary"
 )
 
+// hasTTY checks if /dev/tty is available for interactive prompts.
+// Returns false when running as an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).
+//
+// In test environments, ENTIRE_TEST_TTY overrides the real check:
+//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 → simulate human (TTY available)
+//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0 → simulate agent (no TTY)
+func hasTTY() bool {
+	if v := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY"); v != "" {
+		return v == "1"
+	}
+	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
+	if err != nil {
+		return false
+	}
+	_ = tty.Close()
+	return true
+}
+
 // askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
 // This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
 // Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
 // If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
 func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
+	// In test mode, don't try to interact with the real TTY — just use the default.
+	// ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 simulates "a human is present" for the hasTTY() check
+	// but we can't actually read from the TTY in tests.
+	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
+		return defaultYes
+	}
+
 	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
 	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
 	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
@@ -239,6 +264,19 @@ func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source str
 		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
 	}
 
+	// Fast path: when an agent is committing (ACTIVE session + no TTY), skip
+	// content detection and interactive prompts. The agent can't respond to TTY
+	// prompts and the content detection can miss mid-session work (no shadow
+	// branch yet, transcript analysis may fail). Generate a checkpoint ID and
+	// add the trailer directly.
+	if !hasTTY() {
+		for _, state := range sessions {
+			if state.Phase.IsActive() {
+				return s.addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx, commitMsgFile, state, source)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
 	// Check if any session has new content to condense
 	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
 
@@ -1025,6 +1063,54 @@ func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.
 	return true, nil
 }
 
+// addTrailerForAgentCommit handles the fast path when an agent is committing
+// (ACTIVE session + no TTY). Generates a checkpoint ID and adds the trailer
+// directly, bypassing content detection and interactive prompts.
+func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string, state *SessionState, source string) error {
+	// Use PendingCheckpointID if set, otherwise generate a new one
+	var cpID id.CheckpointID
+	if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
+		var err error
+		cpID, err = id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID)
+		if err != nil {
+			cpID = "" // fall through to generate
+		}
+	}
+	if cpID.IsEmpty() {
+		var err error
+		cpID, err = id.Generate()
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
+		}
+	}
+
+	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
+	}
+
+	message := string(content)
+
+	// Don't add if trailer already exists
+	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
+
+	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: agent commit trailer added",
+		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
+		slog.String("source", source),
+		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
+		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
+	)
+
+	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
+		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.
 // Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).
 func addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {

+// DeleteBranchCLI deletes a git branch using the git CLI.
+// Uses `git branch -D` instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5
+// doesn't properly persist deletions when refs are packed (.git/packed-refs)
+// or in a worktree context. This is the same class of go-git v5 bug that
+// affects checkout and reset --hard (see HardResetWithProtection).
+func DeleteBranchCLI(branchName string) error {
+	ctx := context.Background()
+	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "branch", "-D", branchName)
+	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete branch %s: %s: %w", branchName, strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // HardResetWithProtection performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit.
 // Uses the git CLI instead of go-git because go-git's HardReset incorrectly
 // deletes untracked directories (like .entire/) even when they're in .gitignore.

---

## Code Review: `alex/entire-clean-fixes`

### Overview
Fixes two bugs in `entire clean`: (1) structured logs leaking to stderr, and (2) shadow branches not actually being deleted due to go-git v5's `RemoveReference` failing with packed refs/worktrees. Clean, focused change across 10 files.

### Correctness

- **Logging fix**: Correct. `logging.Init("")` routes logs to `.entire/logs/entire.log`. The `if err == nil { defer Close() }` pattern is a nice touch — non-fatal fallback.

- **Branch deletion fix**: Correct approach, consistent with the documented go-git v5 workaround pattern (`HardResetWithProtection`, `CheckoutBranch`).

- **Idempotent delete handling**: `strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not found")` matches the actual git output (`error: branch 'foo' not found`). Applied consistently in `deleteShadowBranch`, `cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused`, but **not** in `DeleteBranchCLI` itself. This is fine — callers decide their own idempotency semantics.

### Issues Found

1. **Minor: `manual_commit_reset.go` still uses go-git to check existence before CLI delete**
   ```go
   refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   _, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)     // go-git check
   hasShadowBranch := err == nil
   // ...
   if hasShadowBranch {
       if err := DeleteBranchCLI(shadowBranchName); err != nil {  // CLI delete
   ```
   The go-git existence check could have the same stale cache issue that motivated this fix. If go-git says the branch doesn't exist (false negative with packed refs), the delete is silently skipped. Consider using `git branch --list` for the check too. **Low severity** — this path (`entire reset`) is less likely to hit packed refs than `entire clean`, and the worst outcome is a no-op.

2. **Minor: Unused `_ *git.Repository` parameters left as blanked args**
   In `deleteShadowBranch`, `cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused`, and `discardSession`, the `repo` parameter is blanked to `_`. This avoids changing all callers, but leaves dead parameters in the API. Consider a follow-up to clean signatures if these are internal-only. **Non-blocking.**

3. **Minor: `checkpoint/temporary.go` duplicates the CLI deletion pattern**
   `GitStore.DeleteShadowBranch` reimplements `exec.CommandContext(..."git", "branch", "-D"...)` because the `checkpoint` package can't import `strategy`. This is dead code (no callers per the plan) but if it gets used later, the duplication could drift. Consider extracting a shared utility to a lower-level package. **Non-blocking** for dead code.

4. **Nit: Missing gosec nolint on `DeleteBranchCLI` in `common.go`**
   The `//nolint:gosec` comment was stripped by gofmt (line too long). Lint passes today because golangci-lint doesn't flag it, but if gosec config changes this could surface. The same comment survived in `checkpoint/temporary.go`. **Informational only.**

### What's Good

- Comprehensive: all 7 non-test `RemoveReference` call sites were updated
- Test updated to verify via `git branch --list` CLI, matching production code path
- Comments consistently explain the "why" (go-git v5 packed refs bug)
- `strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not found")` idempotent handling is correct and matches actual git output
- Existing tests pass including integration tests (56s suite)
- No behavior change for non-deletion code paths

### Verdict
**Ship it.** The core fix is correct, well-documented, and consistent with established patterns. The minor issues (stale go-git existence check in `Reset`, blanked parameters, dead code duplication) are all low-risk and appropriate for follow-up.

diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/mid_session_commit_test.go b/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/mid_session_commit_test.go
index 5d3c97f3..65437952 100644
--- a/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/mid_session_commit_test.go
+++ b/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/mid_session_commit_test.go
@@ -181,3 +181,32 @@ func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerForUnrelatedFile(t *testing.T)
 		t.Log("Correctly omitted checkpoint trailer for unrelated file commit")
 	}
 }
+
+// TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer tests that when an agent commits
+// (ACTIVE session + no TTY), the trailer is always added regardless of content
+// detection. This is the fast path that bypasses transcript analysis.
+func TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
+
+	session := env.NewSession()
+
+	// Start session (sets phase to ACTIVE)
+	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Create a file and commit as agent (no TTY)
+	env.WriteFile("agent_file.txt", "created by agent")
+	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent("Agent commit", "agent_file.txt")
+
+	// Agent commits should ALWAYS get a trailer (fast path, no content detection)
+	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
+	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
+	if checkpointID == "" {
+		t.Error("Agent commit during ACTIVE session should always get a checkpoint trailer")
+	} else {
+		t.Logf("Agent commit correctly got checkpoint trailer: %s", checkpointID)
+	}
+}

diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go b/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go

 	return []byte(content), nil
 }
 
+// DeleteBranchCLI deletes a git branch using the git CLI.
+// Uses `git branch -D` instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5
+// doesn't properly persist deletions when refs are packed (.git/packed-refs)
+// or in a worktree context. This is the same class of go-git v5 bug that
+// affects checkout and reset --hard (see HardResetWithProtection).
+func DeleteBranchCLI(branchName string) error {
+	ctx := context.Background()
+	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "branch", "-D", branchName)
+	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete branch %s: %s: %w", branchName, strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // HardResetWithProtection performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit.
 // Uses the git CLI instead of go-git because go-git's HardReset incorrectly
 // deletes untracked directories (like .entire/) even when they're in .gitignore.

-	}
-	if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(ref.Name()); err != nil {
-		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch reference: %w", err)
+	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch via CLI
+	// (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)
+	if err := DeleteBranchCLI(shadowBranchName); err != nil {
+		// Branch already gone is not an error
+		if strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not found") {
+			return nil

+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch: %w", err)

diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go b/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go

@@ -25,11 +25,36 @@ import (
 	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/utils/binary"
 )
 
+// hasTTY checks if /dev/tty is available for interactive prompts.
+// Returns false when running as an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).
+//
+// In test environments, ENTIRE_TEST_TTY overrides the real check:
+//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 → simulate human (TTY available)
+//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0 → simulate agent (no TTY)
+func hasTTY() bool {
+	if v := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY"); v != "" {
+		return v == "1"
+	}
+	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
+	if err != nil {
+		return false
+	}
+	_ = tty.Close()
+	return true
+}
+
 // askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
 // This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
 // Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
 // If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
 func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
+	// In test mode, don't try to interact with the real TTY — just use the default.
+	// ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 simulates "a human is present" for the hasTTY() check
+	// but we can't actually read from the TTY in tests.
+	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
+		return defaultYes
+	}
+
 	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
 	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
 	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
@@ -239,6 +264,19 @@ func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source str
 		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
 	}
 
+	// Fast path: when an agent is committing (ACTIVE session + no TTY), skip
+	// content detection and interactive prompts. The agent can't respond to TTY
+	// prompts and the content detection can miss mid-session work (no shadow
+	// branch yet, transcript analysis may fail). Generate a checkpoint ID and
+	// add the trailer directly.
+	if !hasTTY() {
+		for _, state := range sessions {
+			if state.Phase.IsActive() {
+				return s.addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx, commitMsgFile, state, source)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
 	// Check if any session has new content to condense
 	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
 
@@ -1025,6 +1063,54 @@ func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.
 	return true, nil
 }
 
+// addTrailerForAgentCommit handles the fast path when an agent is committing
+// (ACTIVE session + no TTY). Generates a checkpoint ID and adds the trailer
+// directly, bypassing content detection and interactive prompts.
+func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string, state *SessionState, source string) error {
+	// Use PendingCheckpointID if set, otherwise generate a new one
+	var cpID id.CheckpointID
+	if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
+		var err error
+		cpID, err = id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID)
+		if err != nil {
+			cpID = "" // fall through to generate
+		}
+	}
+	if cpID.IsEmpty() {
+		var err error
+		cpID, err = id.Generate()
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
+		}
+	}
+
+	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
+	}
+
+	message := string(content)
+
+	// Don't add if trailer already exists
+	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
+
+	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: agent commit trailer added",
+		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
+		slog.String("source", source),
+		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
+		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
+	)
+
+	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
+		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.
 // Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).
 func addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {

 		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Deleted shadow branch %s\n", shadowBranchName)

To keep discard idempotent (and avoid races between classification and deletion), treat \"branch not found\" as a non-fatal condition here (similar to `deleteShadowBranch` / `cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused`).\n```suggestion\n\t\t\t\t// Treat \"branch not found\" as non-fatal to keep discard idempotent.\n\t\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, git.ErrBranchNotFound) || errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound) {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(errW, \"Warning: shadow branch %s already deleted: %v\\n\", ss.ShadowBranch, err)\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to delete shadow branch: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t\t}\n```"

{
  "id": 2785477259,
  "path": "cmd/entire/cli/strategy/cleanup.go",
  "line": 129,
  "author": "Copilot",
  "body": "The docstring says this returns an error only if the repository cannot be opened, but the function no longer opens the repo and will return `err == nil` even when `git branch -D` fails because the current directory is not a git repo (it just adds to `failed`). Either update the comment/contract, or restore explicit repo detection so callers can distinguish \"not in repo\" from per-branch failures."
}
{
  "id": 2785477268,
  "path": "cmd/entire/cli/clean.go",
  "line": 55,
  "author": "Copilot",
  "body": "`logging.Init` supports reading log level from settings via `logging.SetLogLevelGetter(...)` (see hook logging). `runClean` initializes logging but doesn't set the getter, so `entire clean` won't honor configured log levels unless `ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL` is set. Consider calling `logging.SetLogLevelGetter(GetLogLevel)` before `logging.Init(\"\")` for consistency."
}

  "body": "### Missing \"not found\" handling in `discardSession` branch deletion\n\n**Medium Severity**\n\n<!-- DESCRIPTION START -->\nThe old `discardSession` code silently skipped deletion when the branch reference didn't exist (via a `refErr == nil` guard). The new code calls `strategy.DeleteBranchCLI` directly, which runs `git branch -D` — that command returns an error for non-existent branches. Unlike the other two idempotent call sites (`deleteShadowBranch` and `cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused`), which both check for `strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"not found\")` and return `nil`, `discardSession` propagates the error. This causes a behavioral regression: the `sessions fix` command will now fail if a shadow branch was already removed.\n<!-- DESCRIPTION END -->\n\n<!-- BUGBOT_BUG_ID: cd6ad698-05ab-40e7-9073-e2d2236a2501 -->\n\n<!-- LOCATIONS START\ncmd/entire/cli/sessions_fix.go#L287-L290\ncmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go#L353-L361\ncmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go#L614-L620\nLOCATIONS END -->\n<details>\n<summary>Additional Locations (2)</summary>\n\n- [`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go#L353-L361`](https://github.REDACTED.go#L353-L361)\n- [`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go#L614-L620`](https://github.REDACTED.go#L614-L620)\n\n</details>\n\n<p><a href=\"https://cursor.com/open?REDACTED.REDACTED.REDACTED\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"><picture><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-cursor-dark.png\"><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: light)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-cursor-light.png\"><img alt=\"Fix in Cursor\" width=\"115\" height=\"28\" src=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-cursor-dark.png\"></picture></a>&nbsp;<a href=\"https://cursor.com/agents?REDACTED.REDACTED.REDACTED\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"><picture><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-web-dark.png\"><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: light)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-web-light.png\"><img alt=\"Fix in Web\" width=\"99\" height=\"28\" src=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-web-dark.png\"></picture></a></p>\n\n"

   115→}
   116→
   117→// DeleteShadowBranches deletes the specified branches from the repository.
   118→// Returns two slices: successfully deleted branches and branches that failed to delete.
   119→// Individual branch deletion failures do not stop the operation - all branches are attempted.
   120→// Returns an error only if the repository cannot be opened.
   121→func DeleteShadowBranches(branches []string) (deleted []string, failed []string, err error) {
   122→	if len(branches) == 0 {
   123→		return []string{}, []string{}, nil
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	for _, branch := range branches {
   127→		// Use git CLI to delete branches because go-git v5's RemoveReference
   128→		// doesn't properly persist deletions with packed refs or worktrees
   129→		if err := DeleteBranchCLI(branch); err != nil {
   130→			failed = append(failed, branch)
   131→			continue
   132→		}
   133→
   134→		deleted = append(deleted, branch)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	return deleted, failed, nil
   138→}
   139→

    12→func newCleanCmd() *cobra.Command {
    13→	var forceFlag bool
    14→
    15→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    16→		Use:   "clean",
    17→		Short: "Clean up orphaned entire's data",
    18→		Long: `Remove orphaned entire's data that wasn't cleaned up automatically.
    19→
    20→This command finds and removes orphaned data from any strategy:
    21→
    22→  Shadow branches (entire/<commit-hash>)
    23→    Created by manual-commit strategy. Normally auto-cleaned when sessions
    24→    are condensed during commits.
    25→
    26→  Session state files (.git/entire-sessions/)
    27→    Track active sessions. Orphaned when no checkpoints or shadow branches
    28→    reference them.
    29→
    30→  Checkpoint metadata (entire/checkpoints/v1 branch)
    31→    For auto-commit checkpoints: orphaned when commits are rebased/squashed
    32→    and no commit references the checkpoint ID anymore.
    33→    Manual-commit checkpoints are permanent (condensed history) and are
    34→    never considered orphaned.
    35→
    36→Without --force, shows a preview of items that would be deleted.
    37→With --force, actually deletes the orphaned items.
    38→
    39→The entire/checkpoints/v1 branch itself is never deleted.`,
    40→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    41→			return runClean(cmd.OutOrStdout(), forceFlag)
    42→		},
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	cmd.Flags().BoolVarP(&forceFlag, "force", "f", false, "Actually delete items (otherwise just preview)")
    46→
    47→	return cmd
    48→}
    49→
    50→func runClean(w io.Writer, force bool) error {
    51→	// Initialize logging so structured logs go to .entire/logs/ instead of stderr.
    52→	// Error is non-fatal: if logging init fails, logs go to stderr (acceptable fallback).
    53→	if err := logging.Init(""); err == nil {
    54→		defer logging.Close()
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// List all cleanup items
    58→	items, err := strategy.ListAllCleanupItems()
    59→	if err != nil {
    60→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list orphaned items: %w", err)

     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"

    18→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object"
    19→)
    20→
    21→const (
    22→	// sessionGracePeriod is the minimum age a session must have before it can be
    23→	// considered orphaned. This protects active sessions that haven't created
    24→	// their first checkpoint yet.
    25→	sessionGracePeriod = 10 * time.Minute
    26→)
    27→
    28→// CleanupType identifies the type of orphaned item.
    29→type CleanupType string
    30→
    31→const (
    32→	CleanupTypeShadowBranch CleanupType = "shadow-branch"
    33→	CleanupTypeSessionState CleanupType = "session-state"
    34→	CleanupTypeCheckpoint   CleanupType = "checkpoint"
    35→)
    36→
    37→// CleanupItem represents an orphaned item that can be cleaned up.
    38→type CleanupItem struct {
    39→	Type   CleanupType
    40→	ID     string // Branch name, session ID, or checkpoint ID
    41→	Reason string // Why this item is considered orphaned
    42→}
    43→
    44→// CleanupResult contains the results of a cleanup operation.
    45→type CleanupResult struct {
    46→	ShadowBranches    []string // Deleted shadow branches
    47→	SessionStates     []string // Deleted session state files
    48→	Checkpoints       []string // Deleted checkpoint metadata
    49→	FailedBranches    []string // Shadow branches that failed to delete
    50→	FailedStates      []string // Session states that failed to delete
    51→	FailedCheckpoints []string // Checkpoints that failed to delete
    52→}
    53→
    54→// shadowBranchPattern matches shadow branch names in both old and new formats:
    55→//   - Old format: entire/<commit[:7+]>
    56→//   - New format: entire/<commit[:7+]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>
    57→//
    58→// The pattern requires at least 7 hex characters for the commit, optionally followed
    59→// by a dash and exactly 6 hex characters for the worktree hash.
    60→var shadowBranchPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^entire/[0-9a-fA-F]{7,}(-[0-9a-fA-F]{6})?$`)
    61→
    62→// IsShadowBranch returns true if the branch name matches the shadow branch pattern.
    63→// Shadow branches have the format "entire/<commit-hash>-<worktree-hash>" where the
    64→// commit hash is at least 7 hex characters and worktree hash is 6 hex characters.
    65→// The "entire/checkpoints/v1" branch is NOT a shadow branch.
    66→func IsShadowBranch(branchName string) bool {
    67→	// Explicitly exclude entire/checkpoints/v1
    68→	if branchName == paths.MetadataBranchName {
    69→		return false
    70→	}
    71→	return shadowBranchPattern.MatchString(branchName)
    72→}
    73→
    74→// ListShadowBranches returns all shadow branches in the repository.
    75→// Shadow branches match the pattern "entire/<commit-hash>" (7+ hex chars).
    76→// The "entire/checkpoints/v1" branch is excluded as it stores permanent metadata.
    77→// Returns an empty slice (not nil) if no shadow branches exist.
    78→func ListShadowBranches() ([]string, error) {
    79→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	refs, err := repo.References()
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get references: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	var shadowBranches []string
    90→
    91→	err = refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
    92→		// Only look at branch references
    93→		if !ref.Name().IsBranch() {
    94→			return nil
    95→		}
    96→
    97→		// Extract branch name without refs/heads/ prefix
    98→		branchName := strings.TrimPrefix(ref.Name().String(), "refs/heads/")
    99→
   100→		if IsShadowBranch(branchName) {
   101→			shadowBranches = append(shadowBranches, branchName)
   102→		}
   103→		return nil
   104→	})
   105→	if err != nil {
   106→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate references: %w", err)
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	// Ensure we return empty slice, not nil
   110→	if shadowBranches == nil {
   111→		shadowBranches = []string{}
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	return shadowBranches, nil
   115→}
   116→
   117→// DeleteShadowBranches deletes the specified branches from the repository.
   118→// Returns two slices: successfully deleted branches and branches that failed to delete.
   119→// Individual branch deletion failures do not stop the operation - all branches are attempted.
   120→// Returns an error only if the repository cannot be opened.
   121→func DeleteShadowBranches(branches []string) (deleted []string, failed []string, err error) {
   122→	if len(branches) == 0 {
   123→		return []string{}, []string{}, nil
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	for _, branch := range branches {
   127→		// Use git CLI to delete branches because go-git v5's RemoveReference
   128→		// doesn't properly persist deletions with packed refs or worktrees
   129→		if err := DeleteBranchCLI(branch); err != nil {
   130→			failed = append(failed, branch)
   131→			continue
   132→		}
   133→
   134→		deleted = append(deleted, branch)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	return deleted, failed, nil
   138→}
   139→
   140→// ListOrphanedSessionStates returns session state files that are orphaned.
   141→// A session state is orphaned if:
   142→//   - No checkpoints on entire/checkpoints/v1 reference this session ID
   143→//   - No shadow branch exists for the session's base commit
   144→//
   145→// This is strategy-agnostic as session states are shared by all strategies.
   146→func ListOrphanedSessionStates() ([]CleanupItem, error) {
   147→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   148→	if err != nil {
   149→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	// Get all session states
   153→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
   154→	if err != nil {
   155→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	if len(states) == 0 {
   164→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	// Get all checkpoints to find which sessions have checkpoints
   168→	cpStore := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
   169→
   170→	sessionsWithCheckpoints := make(map[string]bool)
   171→	checkpoints, listErr := cpStore.ListCommitted(context.Background())
   172→	if listErr == nil {
   173→		for _, cp := range checkpoints {
   174→			sessionsWithCheckpoints[cp.SessionID] = true
   175→		}
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	// Get all shadow branches as a set for quick lookup
   179→	shadowBranches, _ := ListShadowBranches() //nolint:errcheck // Best effort
   180→	shadowBranchSet := make(map[string]bool)
   181→	for _, branch := range shadowBranches {
   182→		shadowBranchSet[branch] = true
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	var orphaned []CleanupItem
   186→	now := time.Now()
   187→
   188→	for _, state := range states {
   189→		// Skip sessions that started recently - they may be actively in use
   190→		// but haven't created their first checkpoint yet
   191→		if now.Sub(state.StartedAt) < sessionGracePeriod {
   192→			continue
   193→		}
   194→
   195→		// Check if session has checkpoints on entire/checkpoints/v1
   196→		hasCheckpoints := sessionsWithCheckpoints[state.SessionID]
   197→
   198→		// Check if shadow branch exists for this session's base commit and worktree
   199→		// Shadow branches are now worktree-specific: entire/<commit[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>
   200→		expectedBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   201→		hasShadowBranch := shadowBranchSet[expectedBranch]
   202→
   203→		// Session is orphaned if it has no checkpoints AND no shadow branch
   204→		if !hasCheckpoints && !hasShadowBranch {
   205→			reason := "no checkpoints or shadow branch found"
   206→			orphaned = append(orphaned, CleanupItem{
   207→				Type:   CleanupTypeSessionState,
   208→				ID:     state.SessionID,
   209→				Reason: reason,
   210→			})
   211→		}
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	return orphaned, nil
   215→}
   216→
   217→// DeleteOrphanedSessionStates deletes the specified session state files.
   218→func DeleteOrphanedSessionStates(sessionIDs []string) (deleted []string, failed []string, err error) {
   219→	if len(sessionIDs) == 0 {
   220→		return []string{}, []string{}, nil
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
   224→	if err != nil {
   225→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	for _, sessionID := range sessionIDs {
   229→		if err := store.Clear(context.Background(), sessionID); err != nil {
   230→			failed = append(failed, sessionID)
   231→		} else {
   232→			deleted = append(deleted, sessionID)
   233→		}
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	return deleted, failed, nil
   237→}
   238→
   239→// DeleteOrphanedCheckpoints removes checkpoint directories from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   240→func DeleteOrphanedCheckpoints(checkpointIDs []string) (deleted []string, failed []string, err error) {
   241→	if len(checkpointIDs) == 0 {
   242→		return []string{}, []string{}, nil
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   246→	if err != nil {
   247→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	// Get sessions branch
   251→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   252→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   253→	if err != nil {
   254→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("sessions branch not found: %w", err)
   255→	}
   256→
   257→	parentCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   258→	if err != nil {
   259→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	baseTree, err := parentCommit.Tree()
   263→	if err != nil {
   264→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	// Flatten tree to entries
   268→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   269→	if err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(repo, baseTree, "", entries); err != nil {
   270→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to flatten tree: %w", err)
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	// Remove entries for each checkpoint
   274→	checkpointSet := make(map[string]bool)
   275→	for _, id := range checkpointIDs {
   276→		checkpointSet[id] = true
   277→	}
   278→
   279→	// Find and remove entries matching checkpoint paths
   280→	for path := range entries {
   281→		for checkpointIDStr := range checkpointSet {
   282→			cpID, err := id.NewCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   283→			if err != nil {
   284→				continue // Skip invalid checkpoint IDs
   285→			}
   286→			cpPath := cpID.Path()
   287→			if strings.HasPrefix(path, cpPath+"/") {
   288→				delete(entries, path)
   289→			}
   290→		}
   291→	}
   292→
   293→	// Build new tree
   294→	newTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(repo, entries)
   295→	if err != nil {
   296→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to build tree: %w", err)
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	// Create commit
   300→	commit := &object.Commit{
   301→		Author: object.Signature{
   302→			Name:  "Entire CLI",
   303→			Email: "cli@entire.io",
   304→			When:  parentCommit.Author.When,
   305→		},
   306→		Committer: object.Signature{
   307→			Name:  "Entire CLI",
   308→			Email: "cli@entire.io",
   309→			When:  parentCommit.Committer.When,
   310→		},
   311→		Message:      fmt.Sprintf("Cleanup: removed %d orphaned checkpoints", len(checkpointIDs)),
   312→		TreeHash:     newTreeHash,
   313→		ParentHashes: []plumbing.Hash{ref.Hash()},
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   317→	if err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {
   318→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to encode commit: %w", err)
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	commitHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
   322→	if err != nil {
   323→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to store commit: %w", err)
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	// Update branch reference
   327→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
   328→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   329→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to update branch: %w", err)
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	// All checkpoints deleted successfully
   333→	return checkpointIDs, []string{}, nil
   334→}
   335→
   336→// ListAllCleanupItems returns all orphaned items across all categories.
   337→// It iterates over all registered strategies and calls ListOrphanedItems on those
   338→// that implement OrphanedItemsLister.
   339→// Returns an error if the repository cannot be opened.
   340→func ListAllCleanupItems() ([]CleanupItem, error) {
   341→	var items []CleanupItem
   342→	var firstErr error
   343→
   344→	// Iterate over all registered strategies
   345→	for _, name := range List() {
   346→		strat, err := Get(name)
   347→		if err != nil {
   348→			if firstErr == nil {
   349→				firstErr = err
   350→			}
   351→			continue
   352→		}
   353→
   354→		// Check if strategy implements OrphanedItemsLister
   355→		if lister, ok := strat.(OrphanedItemsLister); ok {
   356→			stratItems, err := lister.ListOrphanedItems()
   357→			if err != nil {
   358→				if firstErr == nil {
   359→					firstErr = err
   360→				}
   361→				continue
   362→			}
   363→			items = append(items, stratItems...)
   364→		}
   365→	}
   366→
   367→	// Orphaned session states (strategy-agnostic)
   368→	states, err := ListOrphanedSessionStates()
   369→	if err != nil {
   370→		if firstErr == nil {
   371→			firstErr = err
   372→		}
   373→	} else {
   374→		items = append(items, states...)
   375→	}
   376→
   377→	return items, firstErr
   378→}
   379→
   380→// DeleteAllCleanupItems deletes all specified cleanup items.
   381→// Logs each deletion for audit purposes.
   382→func DeleteAllCleanupItems(items []CleanupItem) (*CleanupResult, error) {
   383→	result := &CleanupResult{}
   384→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "cleanup")
   385→
   386→	// Build ID-to-Reason map for logging after deletion
   387→	reasonMap := make(map[string]string)
   388→	for _, item := range items {
   389→		reasonMap[item.ID] = item.Reason
   390→	}
   391→
   392→	// Group items by type
   393→	var branches, states, checkpoints []string
   394→	for _, item := range items {
   395→		switch item.Type {
   396→		case CleanupTypeShadowBranch:
   397→			branches = append(branches, item.ID)
   398→		case CleanupTypeSessionState:
   399→			states = append(states, item.ID)
   400→		case CleanupTypeCheckpoint:
   401→			checkpoints = append(checkpoints, item.ID)
   402→		}
   403→	}
   404→
   405→	// Delete shadow branches
   406→	if len(branches) > 0 {
   407→		deleted, failed, err := DeleteShadowBranches(branches)
   408→		if err != nil {
   409→			return result, err
   410→		}
   411→		result.ShadowBranches = deleted
   412→		result.FailedBranches = failed
   413→
   414→		// Log deleted branches
   415→		for _, id := range deleted {
   416→			logging.Info(logCtx, "deleted orphaned shadow branch",
   417→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeShadowBranch)),
   418→				slog.String("id", id),
   419→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   420→			)
   421→		}
   422→		// Log failed branches
   423→		for _, id := range failed {
   424→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "failed to delete orphaned shadow branch",
   425→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeShadowBranch)),
   426→				slog.String("id", id),
   427→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   428→			)
   429→		}
   430→	}
   431→
   432→	// Delete session states
   433→	if len(states) > 0 {
   434→		deleted, failed, err := DeleteOrphanedSessionStates(states)
   435→		if err != nil {
   436→			return result, err
   437→		}
   438→		result.SessionStates = deleted
   439→		result.FailedStates = failed
   440→
   441→		// Log deleted session states
   442→		for _, id := range deleted {
   443→			logging.Info(logCtx, "deleted orphaned session state",
   444→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeSessionState)),
   445→				slog.String("id", id),
   446→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   447→			)
   448→		}
   449→		// Log failed session states
   450→		for _, id := range failed {
   451→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "failed to delete orphaned session state",
   452→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeSessionState)),
   453→				slog.String("id", id),
   454→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   455→			)
   456→		}
   457→	}
   458→
   459→	// Delete checkpoints
   460→	if len(checkpoints) > 0 {
   461→		deleted, failed, err := DeleteOrphanedCheckpoints(checkpoints)
   462→		if err != nil {
   463→			return result, err
   464→		}
   465→		result.Checkpoints = deleted
   466→		result.FailedCheckpoints = failed
   467→
   468→		// Log deleted checkpoints
   469→		for _, id := range deleted {
   470→			logging.Info(logCtx, "deleted orphaned checkpoint",
   471→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeCheckpoint)),
   472→				slog.String("id", id),
   473→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   474→			)
   475→		}
   476→		// Log failed checkpoints
   477→		for _, id := range failed {
   478→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "failed to delete orphaned checkpoint",
   479→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeCheckpoint)),
   480→				slog.String("id", id),
   481→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   482→			)
   483→		}
   484→	}
   485→
   486→	// Log summary
   487→	totalDeleted := len(result.ShadowBranches) + len(result.SessionStates) + len(result.Checkpoints)
   488→	totalFailed := len(result.FailedBranches) + len(result.FailedStates) + len(result.FailedCheckpoints)
   489→	if totalDeleted > 0 || totalFailed > 0 {
   490→		logging.Info(logCtx, "cleanup completed",
   491→			slog.Int("deleted_branches", len(result.ShadowBranches)),
   492→			slog.Int("deleted_session_states", len(result.SessionStates)),
   493→			slog.Int("deleted_checkpoints", len(result.Checkpoints)),
   494→			slog.Int("failed_branches", len(result.FailedBranches)),
   495→			slog.Int("failed_session_states", len(result.FailedStates)),
   496→			slog.Int("failed_checkpoints", len(result.FailedCheckpoints)),
   497→		)
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	return result, nil
   501→}

All done. Here's a summary of what was fixed and pushed:

**PR review feedback addressed in `6a337e0c`:**

1. **`sessions_fix.go`** — Added `"not found"` idempotency handling in `discardSession` so deleting an already-gone shadow branch doesn't return an error (matching the old go-git behavior where `Reference()` silently failed)

2. **`clean.go`** — Added `logging.SetLogLevelGetter(GetLogLevel)` before `logging.Init("")` so the clean command respects the user's log level setting, consistent with `hooks_git_cmd.go`

3. **`cleanup.go`** — Removed the stale docstring line "Returns an error only if the repository cannot be opened" since `DeleteShadowBranches` no longer opens the repo

All 5 PR review threads have been replied to. The duplication concern in `checkpoint/temporary.go` was acknowledged as intentional (import cycle prevents sharing, and it's dead code).

=== Comment 2785533373 ===
{"body":"DeleteBranchCLI invokes `git branch -D` without a `--` separator and without pinning the working directory to the target repo/worktree. If `branchName` ever starts with `-` it can be parsed as an option, and if the process CWD isn’t the intended repo it may delete the wrong ref or fail unexpectedly. Consider adding `--` before the branch name and setting `cmd.Dir` (or using `git -C \u003cworktree\u003e`) via `GetWorktreePath()` so the deletion is scoped to the correct repository.\n```suggestion\n\n\tworktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to determine worktree path: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"branch\", \"-D\", \"--\", branchName)\n\tcmd.Dir = worktreePath\n```","id":2785533373,"line":1115,"path":"cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go"}

{"body":"### Stale go-git cache breaks deletion confirmation in ResetSession\n\n**Low Severity**\n\n\u003c!-- DESCRIPTION START --\u003e\n`cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused` now deletes branches via CLI (`DeleteBranchCLI`), but `ResetSession` at lines 122–123 still verifies deletion using go-git's `repo.Reference()`. > AGENT

Good. Let me analyze the feedback. There are two categories:

1. **Brittle `"not found"` string matching** (comments 2785533331, 2785533380, 2785533389) — Three comments all say the same thing: use a pre-check (`git show-ref --verify`) instead of string matching for idempotency.

2. **Missing `cmd.Dir` and `--` separator** (comments 2785533357, 2785533373) — `DeleteBranchCLI` and `GitStore.DeleteShadowBranch` don't pin working directory or use `--` separator.

3. **Stale go-git cache in `ResetSession`** (comment 2785571976) — `manual_commit_reset.go` still uses `repo.Reference()` to verify deletion after `DeleteBranchCLI`.

Let me evaluate these and implement fixes. Let me read the relevant code sections first.

Now let me implement the fixes. The approach:

1. **`DeleteBranchCLI`**: Add a `git show-ref --verify` pre-check that returns `ErrBranchNotFound` sentinel, plus add `--` separator. Skip `cmd.Dir` since that matches the existing pattern (`HardResetWithProtection` also doesn't set it).

2. **All callers**: Replace `strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not found")` with `errors.Is(err, ErrBranchNotFound)`.

3. **`manual_commit_reset.go`**: Replace `repo.Reference()` verification with CLI-based check.

4. **`checkpoint/temporary.go`**: Add `--` separator and use worktree root for `cmd.Dir` (this one has access to `s.repo`).

{

  "old_string": "// DeleteBranchCLI deletes a git branch using the git CLI.\n// Uses `git branch -D` instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5\n// doesn't properly persist deletions when refs are packed (.git/packed-refs)\n// or in a worktree context. This is the same class of go-git v5 bug that\n// affects checkout and reset --hard (see HardResetWithProtection).\nfunc DeleteBranchCLI(branchName string) error {\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"branch\", \"-D\", branchName)\n\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to delete branch %s: %s: %w\", branchName, strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// ErrBranchNotFound is returned by DeleteBranchCLI when the branch does not exist.\nvar ErrBranchNotFound = errors.New(\"branch not found\")\n\n// DeleteBranchCLI deletes a git branch using the git CLI.\n// Uses `git branch -D` instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5\n// doesn't properly persist deletions when refs are packed (.git/packed-refs)\n// or in a worktree context. This is the same class of go-git v5 bug that\n// affects checkout and reset --hard (see HardResetWithProtection).\n//\n// Returns ErrBranchNotFound if the branch does not exist, allowing callers\n// to use errors.Is for idempotent deletion patterns.\nfunc DeleteBranchCLI(branchName string) error {\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\t// Pre-check: verify the branch exists so callers get a structured error\n\t// instead of parsing git's output string (which varies across locales).\n\tcheck := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"show-ref\", \"--verify\", \"--quiet\", \"refs/heads/\"+branchName) //nolint:gosec // branchName comes from internal shadow branch naming\n\tif err := check.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"%w: %s\", ErrBranchNotFound, branchName)\n\t}\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"branch\", \"-D\", \"--\", branchName) //nolint:gosec // branchName comes from internal shadow branch naming\n\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to delete branch %s: %s: %w\", branchName, strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}"
}

> AGENT

All 6 review threads have been addressed and replied to. Here's a summary:

**Fixes in `cd9b3699`:**

1. **Sentinel error for branch-not-found** — Added `ErrBranchNotFound` sentinel and `git show-ref --verify` pre-check in `DeleteBranchCLI`. All 4 callers now use `errors.Is(err, ErrBranchNotFound)` instead of brittle `strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not found")`.

2. **`--` separator** — Added `--` before branch name in all `git branch -D` calls (both `DeleteBranchCLI` and `GitStore.DeleteShadowBranch`) to prevent branch names starting with `-` from being parsed as options.

3. **Stale go-git cache in `ResetSession`** — Replaced `repo.Reference()` verification with `branchExistsCLI()` (which uses `git show-ref --verify`) so branch deletion confirmation works correctly with packed refs.

4. **`cmd.Dir` skipped intentionally** — Matches the established codebase pattern where `HardResetWithProtection`, `CheckoutBranch`, etc. all rely on process CWD being within the repo.

All 11 review threads across both rounds are now addressed. Would you like to mark the PR as ready for review?

  1111→// DeleteBranchCLI deletes a git branch using the git CLI.
  1112→// Uses `git branch -D` instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5
  1113→// doesn't properly persist deletions when refs are packed (.git/packed-refs)
  1114→// or in a worktree context. This is the same class of go-git v5 bug that
  1115→// affects checkout and reset --hard (see HardResetWithProtection).
  1116→//
  1117→// Returns ErrBranchNotFound if the branch does not exist, allowing callers

  1120→	ctx := context.Background()
  1121→

  1123→	// instead of parsing git's output string (which varies across locales).

  1132→	}
  1133→	return nil
  1134→}
